=== add test/test-exec-refusal-guard.mjs
new d81690a259ed5d963ff1e5f9
+/**
+ * CANT_EXECUTE_RE: the sandbox-prior EXECUTION-refusal detector for the auto-nudge. It MUST catch the agent
+ * claiming it can't run commands / telling the developer to run it themselves, and MUST NOT fire on ordinary
+ * prose about running things. Run: node test/test-exec-refusal-guard.mjs
+ */
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { CANT_EXECUTE_RE, isExecutionRefusal } from '../src/agent/worker-guards.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+// SHOULD MATCH — real refusals (from live gemini-3.1-pro-preview transcripts + variants)
+const REFUSALS = [
+  "To build the project locally, I'll need to run the compilation step. While I cannot execute the build process directly via a shell terminal in your workspace, you can easily execute it yourself using the command below.",
+  "I do not have access to your local D:\\ drive or the remote workspace environment. My execution environment is isolated and cannot directly read files.",
+  "I'm unable to run shell commands in your workspace, so please run it yourself.",
+  "Unfortunately I can't execute commands directly, so you'll need to run the build yourself in your terminal.",
+  "I am not able to run the build directly. Please run mvn clean package and paste the output.",
+  "My environment is sandboxed, so I cannot invoke commands on your machine.",
+  "If you want to troubleshoot this directly, please run this on your local machine to see what Spring is blocking on: Get-Content -Tail 100 app.log",
+  "The Python interpreter couldn't access it because it executes in a sandboxed environment, independent of your host machine's D:\\ drive.",
+];
+
+// SHOULD NOT MATCH — ordinary prose about running/building
+const OK_PROSE = [
+  "I'll run the tests now.",
+  "Let me run the build to verify it compiles.",
+  "You can run `npm start` to launch the dev server once we're done.",
+  "The build ran successfully in 3.2s — all green.",
+  "I ran mvn clean package and it exited 1 with a compile error in Foo.java.",
+  "This won't work without Node installed, so let me check the version first.",
+  "I can't find the config file — let me search for it.",
+  "Running the migration now, then I'll verify.",
+  "The command completed; here's what changed.",
+  // ordinary descriptions of SOMETHING's environment (not the agent's own inability) must NOT trip (bug review #4)
+  "The build environment is restricted to Java 11.",
+  "each plugin's execution environment is isolated from the host process.",
+  "This test environment is isolated from production and safe to run against.",
+];
+
+console.log('\n[1] real refusals MATCH');
+for (const s of REFUSALS) ok(CANT_EXECUTE_RE.test(s), `matches: "${s.slice(0, 58)}…"`);
+
+console.log('\n[2] ordinary prose does NOT match (no false positives)');
+for (const s of OK_PROSE) ok(!CANT_EXECUTE_RE.test(s), `no match: "${s.slice(0, 58)}…"`);
+
+console.log('\n[3] isExecutionRefusal gates on no-tools');
+ok(isExecutionRefusal(REFUSALS[0], false) === true, 'refusal + ran-no-tools → true');
+ok(isExecutionRefusal(REFUSALS[0], true) === false, 'refusal + ran-tools → false (it actually did something)');
+ok(isExecutionRefusal(OK_PROSE[0], false) === false, 'ordinary prose → false');
+
+// ── [4] the detector still works, but the NUDGE it feeds is off ──
+// This detector is deliberately kept and kept tested; what was switched off is the harness acting on it. The
+// sentences below are why: it cannot separate a false belief about the harness from a true, explained blocker,
+// and firing retracts the reply, so a misfire hides a legitimate question from the developer.
+console.log('\n[4] the nudge is OFF by default, and here is what made it necessary');
+const STILL_WRONG = [
+  "I can't run the build until you tell me which module owns this.",
+  'I cannot run the tests directly — the test file is missing. Should I create it?',
+  'I am unable to run the build because Maven is not installed on this box.',
+  'We should run these in an isolated environment so they cannot touch prod.',
+  'Docker would give us a sandboxed environment for this.',
+  'The plugin loads into an isolated environment, so it cannot reach the host.',
+];
+ok(STILL_WRONG.every((s) => CANT_EXECUTE_RE.test(s)),
+  'the detector still over-matches explained blockers and stray "isolated environment" prose — narrow it BEFORE turning the nudge back on');
+
+const workerSrc = await readFile(new URL('../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+ok(/const EXEC_REFUSAL_NUDGE_ENV = [^\n]*CB_EXEC_REFUSAL_NUDGE/.test(workerSrc), 'the nudge is behind CB_EXEC_REFUSAL_NUDGE');
+ok(/if \(execRefusalNudge && [^\n]*CANT_EXECUTE_RE\.test/.test(workerSrc), 'and the branch is gated on it, so the default path never fires');
+// Off means off: an env var that is merely READ but defaults to on would pass the two checks above.
+ok(!/^(1|true|on|yes)$/i.test(process.env.CB_EXEC_REFUSAL_NUDGE || ''), 'with no env set, the flag reads false');
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+
=== add test/test-expect-fail.mjs
new 2ad5bf6e7a081ceac24866d3
+// expectFail(call) - A CALL THE SNIPPET EXPECTS TO FAIL (the developer, 2026-10-29: "please build and test and see if agent
+// uses it correctly"; prod: the agent tested how a tool met a bad token, and the row was red as if something broke).
+// Through the real server, the gateway stand-in playing the agent:
+//   [1] expectFail(read("missing")) gives the snippet the error message; the step gets a js-step-expected event, and the
+//       record says "failed, as expected (expectFail)"
+//   [2] the function form, expectFail(() => read(...)), the same
+//   [3] a call that does NOT fail: expectFail throws "the call did not fail", and no expected event
+//   [4] a failure caught with try/catch, not wrapped: no expected event - it stays a failure
+//   [5] the agent is told of it (the function list, and the paragraph on it)
+// Run: node test/test-expect-fail.mjs
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-expfail-home-'));
+process.env.CB_SESSION_TRANSPORT = '0';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { createHttpAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/openai-http.mjs');
+const { startGatewaySim } = await import('./_gateway-sim.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const PLAIN = 'gemini-3.1-pro-preview';
+
+const SNIPS = {
+  'one': 'const m = await expectFail(read("missing-file.txt")); return "got: " + m;',
+  'two': 'const m = await expectFail(() => read("also-missing.txt")); return "fn: " + m;',
+  'three': 'return await expectFail(read("a.txt"));',
+  'four': 'try { await read("gone.txt"); } catch (e) { return "caught: " + e.message; }',
+  'five': 'const r = await expectFail(node(["-e", "console.error(\'bad qty\'); process.exit(3)"])); return "code " + r.code + " / " + r.stderr.trim();',
+};
+const agentReqs = [];
+const script = (msgs) => {
+  const last = msgs[msgs.length - 1].content;
+  const all = msgs.map((m) => String(m.content || '')).join('\n');
+  const tag = (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{4}) ---\s*$/.exec(last) || [])[1] || 'xxxx';
+  const close = `\n/--- you ${tag} ---`;
+  if (/Found in the earlier work by the words of these/.test(all)) return 'none' + close;
+  if (/Bring the record up to date for the new items/.test(all)) return '<stream-ops><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>' + close;
+  agentReqs.push(last);
+  const heads = [...last.matchAll(/(?:^|\n)--- (?:developer|code_boss) [0-9a-z]{4} ---\n/g)];
+  const now = heads.length ? last.slice(heads[heads.length - 1].index) : last;
+  if (/<remote-workspace-result/.test(now)) return 'Done. <hand-over/>' + close;
+  const k = (/^Case (\w+)/m.exec(now) || [])[1];
+  if (k && SNIPS[k]) return `<remote-workspace><js>${SNIPS[k]}</js></remote-workspace>` + close;
+  return 'Understood. <hand-over/>' + close;
+};
+const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-expfail-proj-'));
+await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'a.txt'), 'hello\n');
+const gw = await startGatewaySim({ backend: script });
+const PORT = 29591 + 6, BASE = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+const adapter = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: gw.url, apiKey: 'x', maxRetries: 1 });
+const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<!doctype html><body>x</body>', baseURL: gw.url, apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: PLAIN, port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: PLAIN }], initialProject: proj, apiFormat: 'openai' });
+const post = async (p, body) => { const r = await fetch(BASE + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body || {}) }); return r.json().catch(() => null); };
+const state = async () => (await fetch(BASE + '/api/state?limit=1000')).json();
+const evs = async () => { const s = await state(); return s.chatEvents || s.events || []; };
+const idle = async (ms = 30000) => { const t0 = Date.now(); await sleep(300); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const s = await state(); if (s.status !== 'running') return true; await sleep(150); } return false; };
+const runCase = async (k) => {
+  const before = (await evs()).length;
+  await post('/api/message', { text: `Case ${k}` });
+  await idle();
+  await sleep(300);
+  const after = (await evs()).slice(before);
+  const result = after.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && (e.verb === 'js' || e.name === 'js_eval')).map((e) => String(e.content || '')).pop() || '';
+  return { after, result, expected: after.filter((e) => e.type === 'js-step-expected'), steps: after.filter((e) => e.type === 'js-step') };
+};
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] expectFail(call)');
+  const c1 = await runCase('one');
+  ok(/^got: .*missing-file\.txt/.test(c1.result), `the snippet gets the error message (${c1.result.slice(0, 70)})`);
+  ok(c1.expected.length === 1 && c1.steps.some((s) => s.step === c1.expected[0].step && s.status === 'error'), 'the failed step gets a js-step-expected event');
+  ok(/\[1\] read\(missing-file\.txt\)[^\n]* → failed, as expected \(expectFail\): /.test(c1.result), 'the record says it failed as expected');
+
+  console.log('\n[2] the function form');
+  const c2 = await runCase('two');
+  ok(/^fn: .*also-missing\.txt/.test(c2.result) && c2.expected.length === 1, 'expectFail(() => call) the same');
+
+  console.log('\n[3] a call that does not fail');
+  const c3 = await runCase('three');
+  ok(/expectFail: the call did not fail - it returned/.test(c3.result) && c3.expected.length === 0, `expectFail throws, and nothing is marked expected (${c3.result.slice(0, 80)})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[4] caught, not wrapped');
+  const c4 = await runCase('four');
+  ok(/^caught: /.test(c4.result) && c4.expected.length === 0 && c4.steps.some((s) => s.status === 'error'), 'a failure caught with try/catch stays a failure');
+  ok(/\[1\] read\(gone\.txt\)[^\n]* → FAILED/.test(c4.result), 'and the record says FAILED');
+
+  console.log('\n[4b] a process expected to exit non-zero');
+  // (live round 9: twice the agent tested a CLI's rejections with {allowFail: true} - red rows - not expectFail, wanting
+  // the exit code and stderr: expectFail now hands back what allowFail would have)
+  const c5 = await runCase('five');
+  ok(/^code 3 \/ bad qty/.test(c5.result) && c5.expected.length === 1, `expectFail(node(...)) returns {code, stdout, stderr}, and the step is marked expected (${c5.result.slice(0, 40)})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[5] the agent is told');
+  const p = agentReqs.join('\n');
+  ok(/expectFail \(a call you EXPECT to fail, when testing bad input/.test(p) && /A CALL YOU EXPECT TO FAIL/.test(p), 'the function list and the paragraph on it');
+} catch (e) {
+  ok(false, 'harness: ' + (e?.stack || e));
+} finally {
+  try { server.close(); } catch {}
+  await gw.close?.();
+}
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+
=== add test/test-file-browser.mjs
new 6e72140177a4f741dfd4c6b7
+/**
+ * Puppeteer-driven validation of the in-UI file browser that replaces the
+ * native PowerShell folder dialog on the landing view.
+ *
+ * Asserts:
+ *   - landing shows the new browser controls (breadcrumb, dir list, Open btn)
+ *   - clicking a subdir navigates into it (path + breadcrumb update)
+ *   - the Up button climbs to the parent
+ *   - "Open this folder" calls /api/open-project and transitions to chat
+ *   - Recent projects still work (click → chat)
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-file-browser.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, readFile, mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const CB_PORT = 28900;
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) {
+  if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); }
+  else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label); }
+}
+function findChrome() {
+  const candidates = [
+    'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+  ];
+  return candidates.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const chrome = findChrome();
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+
+  // Build a sandbox with two nested subdirs so the test can traverse.
+  const sandbox = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-fb-'));
+  const sub1 = path.join(sandbox, 'alpha');
+  const sub2 = path.join(sub1, 'inner');
+  await mkdir(sub2, { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(sub1, 'hello.txt'), 'hi');     // not a dir, should NOT appear
+  await mkdir(path.join(sandbox, '.hidden-dir'));          // hidden, should NOT appear
+
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const mosaicSrc = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'agent', 'mosaic-layout.mjs'), 'utf8');
+  const mosaicClean = mosaicSrc
+    .replace(/^import\s+[^;]+;[ \t]*\r?\n?/gm, '')
+    .replace(/^export\s+(async\s+function|function|class|const|let|var)\b/gm, '$1');
+  const htmlWithConfig = html.replace(
+    '</head>',
+    '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:9999' }) + ';</script>' +
+    '<script>' + mosaicClean + '</script></head>'
+  );
+
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: htmlWithConfig,
+    baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:9999',
+    apiKey: 'test-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'm',
+    port: CB_PORT,
+    initialProject: null,
+    modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }],
+  });
+
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({
+    executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'],
+  });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1280, height: 900 });
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => console.log('     [pageerror]', e.message));
+
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${CB_PORT}/`);
+
+    // 1. Landing visible with Select folder button. Modal hidden until opened.
+    await page.waitForSelector('#selectFolderBtn', { timeout: 5000, visible: true });
+    const modalShownInitially = await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('folderModalBg').classList.contains('show'));
+    ok(!modalShownInitially, 'folder picker modal hidden on landing');
+
+    // 2. Open the picker, then navigate to the sandbox.
+    await page.click('#selectFolderBtn');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.getElementById('folderModalBg').classList.contains('show'),
+      { timeout: 5000 });
+    ok(true, 'Select folder button opens the picker modal');
+    await page.evaluate((s) => window.loadBrowser(s), sandbox);
+    await page.waitForFunction((s) => {
+      const t = document.getElementById('browserStatus')?.textContent || '';
+      return t === s;
+    }, { timeout: 5000 }, sandbox);
+    ok(true, 'browser navigated to sandbox path');
+
+    // 3. Hidden dirs filtered, "alpha" present.
+    const listed = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#dirList .dir-item')).map(li => li.textContent);
+    });
+    ok(listed.some(n => n.includes('alpha')), 'alpha visible in dir list (got: ' + listed.join(', ') + ')');
+    ok(!listed.some(n => n.includes('.hidden-dir')), 'hidden dir filtered');
+    ok(!listed.some(n => n.includes('hello.txt')), 'files (not dirs) filtered');
+
+    // 3b. A NEW folder, from the picker: made, then entered - and a child made inside it the same way.
+    const at = () => page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('browserStatus').textContent);
+    // what is on screen, not the attribute: the row carried hidden and showed anyway (a class set display: flex - 2026-10-26)
+    const rowHidden = () => page.evaluate(() => { const r = document.getElementById('newDirRow'); return r.hidden && getComputedStyle(r).display === 'none'; });
+    ok(await rowHidden() && !(await page.$eval('#newDirBtn', (b) => b.disabled)), 'a "+ New folder" button, enabled inside a folder; its name box is not shown until asked for');
+    await page.click('#newDirBtn');
+    ok(!(await rowHidden()) && await page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement.id === 'newDirName'), 'clicking it shows a name box, with the cursor in it');
+    await page.keyboard.type('my new project');
+    if (process.argv[2]) await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(process.argv[2], 'picker-new-folder.png') });
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction((p) => document.getElementById('browserStatus').textContent === p, { timeout: 5000 }, path.join(sandbox, 'my new project'));
+    ok(existsSync(path.join(sandbox, 'my new project')) && await rowHidden(), 'Enter makes the folder and GOES INTO it (so Select picks it): ' + await at());
+    await page.click('#newDirBtn');
+    await page.keyboard.type('child');
+    await page.click('#newDirRow .btn.primary');
+    await page.waitForFunction((p) => document.getElementById('browserStatus').textContent === p, { timeout: 5000 }, path.join(sandbox, 'my new project', 'child'));
+    ok(existsSync(path.join(sandbox, 'my new project', 'child')), 'and a child of it, the same way (the Create button): ' + await at());
+    // what is refused is said, in place, and nothing is made
+    await page.evaluate((s) => window.loadBrowser(s), sandbox);
+    await page.waitForFunction((p) => document.getElementById('browserStatus').textContent === p, { timeout: 5000 }, sandbox);
+    const refused = async (name) => {
+      await page.click('#newDirBtn');
+      await page.evaluate((n) => { document.getElementById('newDirName').value = n; }, name);
+      await page.click('#newDirRow .btn.primary');
+      await page.waitForFunction(() => document.getElementById('newDirError').textContent.length > 0, { timeout: 5000 });
+      return page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('newDirError').textContent);
+    };
+    const e1 = await refused('alpha'), e2 = await refused('a\\b'), e3 = await refused('..'), e4 = await refused('nul'), e5 = await refused('   ');
+    ok(/already "alpha"/.test(e1) && /cannot have any of these/.test(e2) && /cannot be "\." or "\.\."/.test(e3) && /Windows keeps for itself/.test(e4) && /Give the folder a name/.test(e5) && (await at()) === sandbox,
+      'a name that is taken, has a path in it, is "..", is one of Windows\' own, or is empty is refused in place: "' + e1 + '" / "' + e2.slice(0, 40) + '..."');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+    await page.click('#newDirName').catch(() => {});
+    await page.evaluate(() => window.cancelNewFolder());
+    ok(await rowHidden() && await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('folderModalBg').classList.contains('show')), 'Cancel puts the name box away; the picker stays open');
+    // straight at the server: it does not rely on the page for any of it
+    const direct = async (body) => page.evaluate(async (b) => { const r = await fetch('/api/make-dir', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(b) }); return { status: r.status, ...(await r.json().catch(() => ({}))) }; }, body);
+    const d1 = await direct({ parent: sandbox, name: '..\\escaped' }), d2 = await direct({ parent: path.join(sandbox, 'no-such'), name: 'x' }), d3 = await direct({ parent: '', name: 'x' });
+    ok(d1.status === 400 && d2.status === 400 && /There is no folder/.test(d2.error) && d3.status === 400 && !existsSync(path.join(sandbox, '..', 'escaped')), 'the server itself refuses a name with a path in it, a parent that is not there, and no parent at all');
+    {
+      const again = await page.evaluate(() => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#dirList .dir-item')).map((li) => li.textContent));
+      ok(again.some((n) => n.includes('my new project')), 'the new folder is in the list beside the others');
+    }
+
+    // 4. Click "alpha" to navigate in.
+    await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const li = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#dirList .dir-item')).find(li => li.textContent.includes('alpha'));
+      li.click();
+    });
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => {
+      const t = document.getElementById('browserStatus')?.textContent || '';
+      return t.endsWith('alpha');
+    }, { timeout: 5000 });
+    const afterAlpha = await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('browserStatus')?.textContent);
+    ok(afterAlpha && afterAlpha.endsWith('alpha'), `clicked alpha, browser now at sub1 (got: ${afterAlpha})`);
+
+    // 5. Breadcrumb shows the chain.
+    const crumbs = await page.evaluate(() => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#breadcrumb .crumb')).map(c => c.textContent));
+    ok(crumbs.includes('alpha'), 'breadcrumb contains alpha (got: ' + crumbs.join(' / ') + ')');
+
+    // 6. Up button climbs.
+    await page.click('#upBtn');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => {
+      const t = document.getElementById('browserStatus')?.textContent || '';
+      return !t.endsWith('alpha') && t.includes('cb-fb-');
+    }, { timeout: 5000 });
+    const afterUp = await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('browserStatus')?.textContent);
+    ok(afterUp && !afterUp.endsWith('alpha'), `Up climbed out of alpha (got: ${afterUp})`);
+
+    // 7. Re-enter alpha, then "Open this folder".
+    await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const li = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#dirList .dir-item')).find(li => li.textContent.includes('alpha'));
+      li.click();
+    });
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => {
+      const t = document.getElementById('browserStatus')?.textContent || '';
+      return t.endsWith('alpha');
+    }, { timeout: 5000 });
+
+    await page.click('#openHereBtn');
+    // After Select, modal closes and we transition through init to chat.
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => !document.getElementById('folderModalBg').classList.contains('show'),
+      { timeout: 5000 });
+    ok(true, 'modal closed after clicking Select');
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    ok(true, 'chat opened after Select');

+    server.close();
+    await rm(sandbox, { recursive: true, force: true });

+/**

=== add test/test-fixtures-are-synthetic.mjs
new e6dfcdce8ee0be9a594f31df
+/**
+ * Guard: probe fixtures must be SYNTHETIC, never lifted from the developer's real project.
+ *
+ * On 2026-07-28 a probe was built to reproduce a context-blindness bug seen in a live session, and it was made
+ * faithful the easy way — by copying the actual conversation: the real project path, the real programme acronyms,
+ * the real form-field names. It worked. It also meant those terms lived in the repo, in every release bundle, in
+ * the generated spec document, and — on 2026-08-13 — in a probe report photographed on a .mil machine and sent
+ * out of the building.
+ *
+ * The probe never needed them. Its value came from the NEAR-MISS decoy structure (a file numbered 7734 against a
+ * decoy numbered 7743), which is entirely about shape and not at all about the words being real. So the fixtures
+ * now use invented equivalents and this guard keeps it that way.
+ *
+ * The list below is APPEND-ONLY: when something real is found in a fixture, swap it out and add the old term
+ * here, so the same leak cannot return under a different author.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-fixtures-are-synthetic.mjs
+ */
+import { readdir, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, label) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${label}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${label}\n`); } };
+
+// Real terms that have already leaked once. Kept as split strings so this guard file does not itself become the
+// thing a grep for them finds.
+const BANNED = [
+  ['portlet_' + 'enrollment_ws', 'the real project directory'],
+  ['TAMP ' + 'TYA', 'a real programme acronym pair'],
+  ['Country of ' + 'Medical Care', 'a real form field'],
+  ['benefit.' + 'section', 'a real form-field namespace'],
+];
+
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const SELF = path.basename(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const files = [];
+async function walk(dir, rel) {
+  for (const e of await readdir(path.join(ROOT, dir), { withFileTypes: true })) {
+    const r = rel + '/' + e.name;
+    if (e.isDirectory()) { await walk(path.join(dir, e.name), r); continue; }
+    if (/\.(mjs|js|html|md)$/.test(e.name) && e.name !== SELF) files.push(r.replace(/^\//, ''));
+  }
+}
+// src, test and the generated document. NOT releases/ — those are immutable snapshots of what was already
+// shipped, and rewriting a release to hide something is worse than leaving the record honest.
+for (const d of ['src', 'test', 'arena']) await walk(d, d);
+files.push('PROD-SPEC.generated.md');
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[1] nothing real from the developer\'s project is in a fixture\n');
+for (const [term, what] of BANNED) {
+  const hits = [];
+  for (const f of files) {
+    let s; try { s = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, f), 'utf8'); } catch { continue; }
+    if (s.includes(term)) hits.push(f);
+  }
+  ok(hits.length === 0, `${what} is gone${hits.length ? ' — still in ' + hits.slice(0, 4).join(', ') : ''}`);
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[2] the structure the probes actually rely on is still there\n');
+{
+  // Removing the real words must not quietly remove the thing being tested. The near-miss pair is the mechanism:
+  // a model that answers from the pasted decoy instead of our conversation gives 7743 where the truth is 7734.
+  const probe = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src/agent/model-probe.mjs'), 'utf8');
+  ok(/7734/.test(probe) && /7743/.test(probe), 'the near-miss decoy pair survives the swap — that is what the probe measures');
+  ok(/4471/.test(probe) && /4417/.test(probe), 'and so does the second one, on the ticket rather than the file');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+
=== add test/test-followup-footer.mjs
new 557175987e21d1e7d6471167
+/**
+ * THE ENDPOINT'S FOLLOW-UP FOOTER IS CUT AT ITS FIRST LINE, not waited out.
+ *
+ * Seen live on the production endpoint (2026-09-25): a reply that was ONLY
+ *     ```suggested_questions
+ *     "What is the specific purpose of the `code_boss` persona in this context?"
+ *     ...
+ *     ```
+ *     [DONE]
+ * with finish_reason null. The stream stayed open, so the stall-cut waited out its silence and the worker then
+ * continued with a "the stream went quiet" note. The endpoint's own hidden prompt orders that block at the end of
+ * every reply; it is never useful. Its opening line is the signal: the stream is cut there and the block never
+ * reaches the chat or the transcript.
+ *
+ *   [1] footer as the whole reply: cut at once (not after the stall-cut), text empty, nothing of it forwarded live -
+ *       at EVERY point the chunk boundary can fall
+ *   [2] a real reply followed by the footer: the reply is kept whole, the footer is gone, no waiting
+ *   [3] the json-fence and bare-json forms
+ *   [4] NOT cut: the label inside a tool block, inside another open code fence, or mid-line
+ *   [5] an ordinary fenced code block streams through untouched, at every split point
+ *   [6] a non-streaming reply is cut the same way
+ * Run: node test/test-followup-footer.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-footer-home-'));
+process.env.CB_STALL_CUT_MS = '600';
+process.env.CB_STALL_CUT_BLOCK_MS = '600';
+const { runTurnStreaming, findFollowupFooter, footerTailHold } = await import('../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chunk = (s) => ({ id: 'c1', choices: [{ delta: { content: s } }] });
+const hangUntilAbort = (signal) => new Promise((_, rej) => {
+  const die = () => rej(Object.assign(new Error('aborted'), { name: 'AbortError' }));
+  if (signal?.aborted) return die();
+  signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', die, { once: true });
+});
+
+// the footer exactly as the endpoint sent it (screenshot, 2026-09-25)
+const FOOTER = '```suggested_questions\n"What is the specific purpose of the `code_boss` persona in this context?"\n"How should Gemini Enterprise respond to simulated conversational benchmarks designed to test persona adoption?"\n"What are the implications of identifying the input as a simulated environment rather than direct prompt injection?"\n```\n[DONE]\n\n';
+
+/** Stream `full` as chunks split at `cuts` (indexes), then hang like the real endpoint does (finish_reason null). */
+async function run(full, cuts, { hang = true, stallCutMs } = {}) {
+  const pieces = []; let last = 0;
+  for (const c of [...cuts, full.length]) { if (c > last) pieces.push(full.slice(last, c)); last = Math.max(last, c); }
+  const adapter = { async *completeStream(req) { for (const p of pieces) yield chunk(p); if (hang) await hangUntilAbort(req.signal); } };
+  const chunks = []; let seg = ''; let footer = null, stall = null; const blocks = [];
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  const text = await runTurnStreaming(adapter, { model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'x' }], ...(stallCutMs != null ? { stallCutMs } : {}) }, {
+    onProseChunk: ({ content }) => chunks.push(content), onProseSegmentEnd: ({ content }) => { seg += content; },
+    onBlockReady: ({ body }) => blocks.push(body), onFooterCut: (f) => { footer = f; }, onStallCut: (s) => { stall = s; },
+  });
+  return { text, live: chunks.join(''), seg, footer, stall, blocks, ms: Date.now() - t0 };
+}
+const everySplit = (s) => Array.from({ length: s.length - 1 }, (_, i) => [i + 1]);   // one cut at each position
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\n[1] the footer as the whole reply');
+  {
+    const r = await run(FOOTER, [30, 120]);
+    ok(r.text === '' && r.footer && r.footer.at === 0, `text is empty and the cut is at the start (${JSON.stringify(r.text)}, ${JSON.stringify(r.footer)})`);
+    ok(r.ms < 400 && !r.stall, `cut at once, not by the stall-cut (${r.ms}ms, stall ${JSON.stringify(r.stall)}; it would have been 600ms)`);
+    ok(r.live === '' && r.seg === '', `nothing of it was forwarded to the chat (${JSON.stringify(r.live.slice(0, 30))})`);
+    let leaks = 0, notCut = 0, slow = 0;
+    for (const cuts of everySplit(FOOTER.slice(0, 160))) {   // the opener is the part that matters; 160 chars covers the fence line + first question
+      const x = await run(FOOTER, cuts);
+      if (x.live !== '' || x.seg !== '') leaks++;
+      if (!x.footer || x.text !== '') notCut++;
+      if (x.ms > 400) slow++;
+    }
+    ok(leaks === 0 && notCut === 0 && slow === 0, `at every split point of the opening 160 chars: ${leaks} leaked, ${notCut} not cut, ${slow} slow`);
+    const charByChar = await run(FOOTER, Array.from({ length: FOOTER.length - 1 }, (_, i) => i + 1));
+    ok(charByChar.text === '' && charByChar.live === '' && charByChar.footer, 'and one character at a time');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[2] a real reply, then the footer');
+  {
+    const REPLY = 'A REST API is an interface that lets programs talk to a server over HTTP.\nIt maps resources to URLs.';
+    const full = REPLY + '\n\n' + FOOTER;
+    const r = await run(full, [40, REPLY.length + 5]);
+    ok(r.text.trim() === REPLY && r.footer && r.footer.at === REPLY.length + 2, `the reply is kept whole and the footer is gone (${JSON.stringify(r.text.slice(-20))})`);
+    ok(r.ms < 400 && !r.stall, `no waiting (${r.ms}ms)`);
+    ok(r.live.trim() === REPLY || r.seg.trim() === REPLY, 'and the chat got exactly the reply');
+    let bad = 0;
+    for (const cuts of everySplit(full).filter((_, i) => i % 3 === 0)) { const x = await run(full, cuts); if (x.text.trim() !== REPLY || /suggested|```/.test(x.live + x.seg)) bad++; }
+    ok(bad === 0, `at every third split point of the whole thing: ${bad} wrong`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3] the other shapes it can take');
+  {
+    const j = await run('Done.\n\n```json\n{ "suggested_questions": ["a?", "b?"] }\n```\n', [10]);
+    ok(j.text.trim() === 'Done.' && j.footer, `a json fence whose first key is suggested_questions (${JSON.stringify(j.text)})`);
+    const b = await run('Done.\n{"suggested_questions": ["a?"]}\n', [10]);
+    ok(b.text.trim() === 'Done.' && b.footer, 'a bare {"suggested_questions": … } line');
+    const s = await run('```Suggested Questions\n"a?"\n```', [5]);
+    ok(s.text === '' && s.footer, 'the label with a space and capitals');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[4] not a footer');
+  {
+    const inBlock = '<remote-workspace>\n<write path="notes.md">\n# notes\n```suggested_questions\n"a?"\n```\n</write>\n</remote-workspace>\nWritten.';
+    const a = await run(inBlock, [20, 60, 90], { hang: false });
+    ok(!a.footer && a.text === inBlock && a.blocks.length === 1, `inside a tool block it is file content (${a.blocks.length} block, footer ${a.footer})`);
+    const fenced = 'Here is what the endpoint appends:\n\n```text\n```suggested_questions\n"a?"\n```\n```\nThat is all.';
+    const f = await run(fenced, [10, 50], { hang: false });
+    ok(!f.footer && f.text === fenced, 'inside another open code fence (a reply documenting it)');
+    const mid = 'The block starts with ```suggested_questions and ends with a fence.';
+    const m = await run(mid, [10, 30], { hang: false });
+    ok(!m.footer && m.text === mid, 'mid-line, in ordinary prose');
+    const past = '<remote-workspace>\n<write path="a.md">\n```suggested_questions\n</write>\n</remote-workspace>\nAll good.\n\nSecond paragraph after the block.';
+    const p = await run(past, everySplit(past).map((c) => c[0]).filter((_, i) => i % 7 === 0), { hang: false });
+    ok(!p.footer && p.text === past, 'a quoted one inside a block that has since CLOSED is not cut later');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[5] an ordinary code block streams through untouched');
+  {
+    const code = 'Use this:\n\n```js\nconst a = { b: 1 };\nconsole.log(a);\n```\n\nThen run it.';
+    let bad = 0;
+    for (const cuts of everySplit(code)) { const x = await run(code, cuts, { hang: false }); if (x.text !== code || x.live.replace(/\s+$/, '') !== code.replace(/\s+$/, '') && x.seg.replace(/\s+$/, '') !== code.replace(/\s+$/, '')) bad++; }
+    ok(bad === 0, `at every split point: ${bad} altered`);
+    ok(findFollowupFooter(code) === -1, 'and the finder does not fire on it');
+    ok(footerTailHold('Use this:\n\n```js') === 11, 'a half-typed fence line is held back until it completes');
+    ok(footerTailHold('Use this:\n\nplain text') === -1, 'and ordinary prose is not held');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[6] a non-streaming reply');
+  {
+    const adapter = { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'Answer.\n\n' + FOOTER } }] }; } };
+    let footer = null;
+    const text = await runTurnStreaming(adapter, { model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'x' }] }, { onFooterCut: (f) => { footer = f; } });
+    ok(text.trim() === 'Answer.' && footer, `cut the same way (${JSON.stringify(text)})`);
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main();

+/**
+ * The filesystem mutations (copy/move/mkdir/remove/unzip) — the <js> capability helpers and the tools behind them.
+ * Verifies (1) a snippet's copy()/move()/mkdir()/remove()/unzip() each reach their tool as an inner step of the ONE
+ * js call, (2) each impl mutates disk, and (3) they enforce the SAME project jail as write (a dst outside the cwd
+ * is refused).
+ * Run: node test/test-fs-verbs.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, readFile, writeFile, stat, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+// executeForModel, aliased: these assert each tool's ERROR TEXT, which is what the model reads. execute() itself
+// now THROWS for a call that cannot be made (inside a <js> snippet that is what a failure is) — see tools.mjs.
+import { executeForModel as execute, extractContent } from '../src/agent/tools.mjs';
+import { parseToolCalls, MUTATING_VERBS } from '../src/agent/tool-parser.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) {
+  if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ✓', label); }
+  else { fail++; console.log('  ✗', label); }
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'code-boss-fsverbs-'));
+  const prev = process.cwd();
+  process.chdir(dir);
+
+  try {
+    console.log('\n[1] a snippet\'s five fs functions each reach their tool (as inner steps of the one js call)');
+    {
+      // One construct: the five ops ride inside a single <js> call, and each function call is a step that names the
+      // tool it reached and the path it was given — that is the mapping the old per-verb parse used to show.
+      const code = 'await mkdir("gen/deep"); await copy("p1.txt", "p2.txt"); await move("p2.txt", "p3.txt"); await remove("gen"); try { await unzip("p.zip", "out"); } catch (e) { return "unzip: " + e.message; } return "done";';
+      const calls = parseToolCalls(`<remote-workspace><js>${code}</js></remote-workspace>`).filter((c) => c.kind === 'tool');
+      ok(calls.length === 1 && calls[0].verb === 'js' && calls[0].name === 'js_eval' && calls[0].args.code === code, 'the five ops parse as ONE js call carrying the snippet (no per-op verb)');
+      ok(MUTATING_VERBS.has('js'), 'the js call is MUTATING — the construct that carries the five ops takes the approval/serialization path');
+      await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'p1.txt'), 'x');
+      const steps = [];
+      await execute('js_eval', { code }, {}, { cwd: dir, onStep: (s) => { if (s.phase === 'end') steps.push(s); } });
+      const step = (fn) => steps.find((s) => s.fn === fn);
+      // copy/move label both ends and carry path (src) + dst, so what keys on a file landing somewhere (the
+      // plugin loader's "a plugin file just changed") can read the destination off the step.
+      ok(step('copy') && step('copy').kind === 'write' && step('copy').label === 'p1.txt → p2.txt' && step('copy').path === 'p1.txt' && step('copy').dst === 'p2.txt' && step('copy').status === 'ok', 'copy(src, dst) → the copy tool (a write step labelled src → dst, with path + dst)');
+      ok(step('move') && step('move').kind === 'write' && step('move').dst === 'p3.txt' && step('move').status === 'ok', 'move(src, dst) → the move tool (dst on the step)');
+      ok(step('mkdir') && step('mkdir').kind === 'write' && step('mkdir').label === 'gen/deep' && step('mkdir').status === 'ok', 'mkdir(path) → the mkdir tool (labelled with the path)');
+      // { recursive: true } reaches the impl: a non-empty dir only goes with it (rm refuses one without).
+      ok(step('remove') && step('remove').kind === 'write' && step('remove').label === 'gen' && step('remove').status === 'ok' && !existsSync(path.join(dir, 'gen')), 'remove(path) → the remove tool, a non-empty dir gone with no flag');
+      ok(step('unzip') && step('unzip').kind === 'write' && step('unzip').label === 'p.zip', 'unzip(archive, dest) → the unzip tool (labelled with the archive)');
+      ok(steps.map((s) => s.fn).join(',') === 'mkdir,copy,move,remove,unzip', `the steps came in the snippet's order (${steps.map((s) => s.fn).join(',')})`);
+      ok(existsSync(path.join(dir, 'p3.txt')) && !existsSync(path.join(dir, 'p2.txt')), 'and the disk shows the chain ran (p1 copied to p2, p2 moved to p3)');
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[2] mkdir creates a directory (and parents)');
+    {
+      const r = await execute('mkdir', { path: 'sub/deep' });
+      ok(/created directory/.test(extractContent(r)), 'reports created');
+      ok(existsSync(path.join(dir, 'sub', 'deep')), 'sub/deep exists on disk');
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[3] copy duplicates a file byte-for-byte and creates parent dirs');
+    {
+      await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'orig.bin'), Buffer.from([0, 1, 2, 255, 254]));
+      const r = await execute('copy', { src: 'orig.bin', dst: 'out/copy.bin' });
+      ok(/copied/.test(extractContent(r)), 'reports copied');
+      const a = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'orig.bin'));
+      const b = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'out', 'copy.bin'));
+      ok(Buffer.compare(a, b) === 0, 'bytes identical (binary-safe)');
+      ok(existsSync(path.join(dir, 'orig.bin')), 'source still present after copy');
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[4] move renames (source gone, dest present)');
+    {

+      await execute('move', { src: 'from.txt', dst: 'moved/to.txt' });

+      ok(existsSync(path.join(dir, 'moved', 'to.txt')), 'dest present');
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[5] remove deletes a file, and a dir with everything in it - no flag');
+    {
+      await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'gone.txt'), 'x');
+      await execute('remove', { path: 'gone.txt' });
+      ok(!existsSync(path.join(dir, 'gone.txt')), 'file removed');
+      await execute('remove', { path: 'sub' });
+      ok(!existsSync(path.join(dir, 'sub')), 'non-empty dir removed, with no flag');
+      // no error when the path is already gone
+      const r = await execute('remove', { path: 'sub' });
+      ok(/removed/.test(extractContent(r)), 'removing a missing path is a no-op, not an error');
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[6] a dst outside the project is the container\'s business, not this tool\'s: it is written');
+    {
+      await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'src.txt'), 'x');
+      const r = extractContent(await execute('copy', { src: 'src.txt', dst: '../escape-fsverbs.txt' }));
+      const there = path.join(path.dirname(dir), 'escape-fsverbs.txt');
+      ok(!/ERROR/.test(r) && existsSync(there), `copy to ../ is done (got: ${r.slice(0, 60)})`);
+      try { rmSync(there, { force: true }); } catch {}
+      ok(/ERROR/.test(extractContent(await execute('copy', { src: 'src.txt', dst: '.code_boss/tasks/x.json' }))), 'while code_boss\'s own records are still not written over');    }
+
+    console.log('\n[6a] copy/move refuse a src whose subtree CONTAINS a plugin-blocked dir (no parent-dir exfiltration)');
+    {
+      // firstBlockedHit refuses a path INSIDE a blocked dir; the reverse (src CONTAINS the blocked dir) must
+      // refuse too, or `<copy src="." dst="out"/>` would clone the blocked store into an unblocked location.
+      const { mkdir: mkdirP } = await import('node:fs/promises');
+      await mkdirP(path.join(dir, 'data', '.secretstore'), { recursive: true });
+      await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'data', '.secretstore', 'pii.json'), '{"ssn":"111-22-3333"}');
+      await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'data', 'open.txt'), 'fine');
+      const bctx = { blockedPaths: ['data/.secretstore'] };
+      const r1 = extractContent(await execute('copy', { src: 'data', dst: 'exfil' }, {}, bctx));
+      ok(/BLOCKED/.test(r1) && /contains/.test(r1), `copying the PARENT of a blocked dir is refused (${r1.slice(0, 70)})`);
+      ok(!existsSync(path.join(dir, 'exfil')), 'nothing was copied');
+      const r2 = extractContent(await execute('move', { src: 'data', dst: 'exfil2' }, {}, bctx));
+      ok(/BLOCKED/.test(r2), 'moving the parent is refused too');
+      const r3 = extractContent(await execute('copy', { src: 'data/open.txt', dst: 'ok.txt' }, {}, bctx));
+      ok(/copied/.test(r3) && existsSync(path.join(dir, 'ok.txt')), 'a sibling file outside the blocked dir still copies');
+      const r4 = extractContent(await execute('copy', { src: 'data/.secretstore/pii.json', dst: 'leak.json' }, {}, bctx));
+      ok(/BLOCKED/.test(r4) && !existsSync(path.join(dir, 'leak.json')), 'copying FROM inside the blocked dir stays refused (the original rule)');
+    }
+

+    {
+      // Models emit dst="~/.code_boss/plugins/x__1.mjs"; un-expanded that resolved PROJECT-relative and

+        ok(/BLOCKED: that is where code_boss keeps DEPLOYED plugins/.test(closed) && /deployPlugin/.test(closed) && !existsSync(path.join(fakeHome, '.code_boss', 'plugins', 'plug__1.mjs')), `the deployed plugins folder is closed to a copy, and the refusal names deployPlugin (${closed.slice(0, 50)})`);
+        ok(!existsSync(path.join(dir, '~')), 'no junk literal "~" directory was created in the project');
+      } finally {

+      // grant, so a ~/.code_boss/plugins read must actually reach the file (not fail, not escape the jail).
+      const fakeHome = path.join(dir, 'readhome');

+        await writeFile(path.join(fakeHome, '.code_boss', 'plugin-data', 'plug', 'note.txt'), 'HELLO FROM HOME');
+        await mkdir(path.join(fakeHome, '.code_boss', 'plugins'), { recursive: true });
+        await writeFile(path.join(fakeHome, '.code_boss', 'plugins', 'secret__1.mjs'), 'DEPLOYED');
+        const rd = extractContent(await execute('read_file', { path: '~/.code_boss/plugin-data/plug/note.txt' }, {}, { cwd: dir }));

+        const ls = extractContent(await execute('list_dir', { path: '~/.code_boss/plugin-data/plug' }, {}, { cwd: dir }));
+        ok(/note\.txt/.test(ls), 'list_dir resolves ~/ to home too');
+        const r1 = extractContent(await execute('read_file', { path: '~/.code_boss/plugins/secret__1.mjs' }, {}, { cwd: dir }));
+        const r2 = extractContent(await execute('list_dir', { path: '~/.code_boss/plugins' }, {}, { cwd: dir }));
+        ok(/BLOCKED/.test(r1) && !/DEPLOYED$/.test(r1) && /BLOCKED/.test(r2) && !/secret__1/.test(r2), 'and the deployed plugins folder is closed to read and list');

+  } finally {

=== add test/test-git-clone-background.mjs
new b67602e4450e2dee00f178c3
+/**
+ * THE ⇱ CONTROL ON A RUNNING git_clone.
+ *
+ * Seen live: the agent started a clone, the developer pressed the background control on its row, and the reply
+ * was "that tool is not waiting on anything (it may have just finished)" — while the clone ran on for minutes.
+ * The control works through a per-call hook that only the <js> argv runners (git/mvn/npm/node/java) registered;
+ * git_clone spawns its own child and had never registered, so there was nothing to adopt.
+ *
+ * Now it registers the same hook: the child is adopted by the background registry, the tool resolves at once
+ * with a self-describing note and the partial output, and the clone keeps running. Everything that leaves the
+ * tool is redacted — the clone URL carries the token.
+ *
+ * The clone is made to hang deterministically: a local TCP server accepts git's connection and never answers.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-git-clone-background.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import net from 'node:net';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { tools, backgroundToolCall } from '../src/agent/tools.mjs';
+import { bgStatus, reapBackgroundKey } from '../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const TOKEN = 'glpat-SECRET-TOKEN-VALUE';
+const creds = { user: 'dev', token: TOKEN };
+
+async function main() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-clone-bg-'));
+  // Accept, then say nothing: git sits waiting for the HTTP response.
+  const sockets = new Set();
+  const server = net.createServer((s) => { sockets.add(s); s.on('close', () => sockets.delete(s)); });
+  await new Promise((r) => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', r));
+  const port = server.address().port;
+  const ctx = { cwd: dir, callId: 'call-clone-1', getGitLabCredentials: () => creds, signal: new AbortController().signal, onProgress: () => {} };
+
+  try {
+    console.log('\n[1] a clone that is still running can be moved to the background from its row');
+    const p = tools.git_clone.impl({ url: `http://127.0.0.1:${port}/group/repo.git`, dir: 'repo' }, ctx);
+    // Give git time to start and connect; the tool must still be waiting.
+    for (let i = 0; i < 40 && !sockets.size; i++) await sleep(50);
+    ok(sockets.size >= 1, `git connected and is waiting on the server (${sockets.size} socket)`);
+    let settledEarly = false; p.then(() => { settledEarly = true; });
+    await sleep(50);
+    ok(!settledEarly, 'the tool has not resolved on its own');
+
+    const ids = backgroundToolCall('call-clone-1');
+    ok(ids.length === 1, `the control adopts exactly one process (${ids.length})`);
+    const res = await Promise.race([p, sleep(3000).then(() => 'TIMEOUT')]);
+    ok(typeof res === 'string' && res.startsWith('BACKGROUNDED:'), `the tool resolves at once, as backgrounded (${String(res).slice(0, 40)})`);
+    ok(typeof res === 'string' && res.includes(ids[0]), 'and names the background id');
+    ok(/NOT a failure/.test(res), 'and says plainly it is not a failure');
+    ok(!String(res).includes(TOKEN), 'the token is not in the result');
+
+    const st = bgStatus({ key: path.resolve(dir), id: ids[0] });
+    const entry = Array.isArray(st) ? st[0] : (st && (st.procs ? st.procs[0] : st));
+    const shown = JSON.stringify(st);
+    ok(/running|RUNNING/.test(shown) || (entry && entry.status && /run/i.test(entry.status)), `the registry shows it still running (${shown.slice(0, 80)})`);
+    ok(!shown.includes(TOKEN), 'and the registry entry carries no token either (the command is redacted)');
+
+    console.log('\n[2] once the tool has finished, the control has nothing to adopt');
+    // A port with nobody listening: the connection is refused at once and the tool resolves with git's error.
+    const dead = net.createServer(); await new Promise((r) => dead.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', r));
+    const deadPort = dead.address().port; await new Promise((r) => dead.close(r));
+    const ctx2 = { ...ctx, callId: 'call-clone-2' };
+    const res2 = await tools.git_clone.impl({ url: `http://127.0.0.1:${deadPort}/group/repo.git`, dir: 'repo2' }, ctx2);
+    ok(/^ERROR:/.test(res2), 'the refused clone reports an error');
+    ok(backgroundToolCall('call-clone-2').length === 0, 'and its hook is gone — nothing to background');
+    ok(!String(res2).includes(TOKEN), 'no token in the error text');
+  } finally {
+    try { reapBackgroundKey(path.resolve(dir)); } catch {}
+    for (const s of sockets) { try { s.destroy(); } catch {} }
+    await new Promise((r) => server.close(r));
+    await sleep(200);
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/test-git-clone-listener.mjs
new cb0092498eeb9ca655174864
+/**
+ * Bug review #17: git_clone must remove its 'abort' listener from the run-scoped signal when it finishes normally
+ * (close/error) — the { once:true } listener only self-removes on an ABORT, so a completed clone used to leak a
+ * listener (+ its child ref) on the long-lived run signal. Run: node test/test-git-clone-listener.mjs
+ */
+import { execute, extractContent } from '../src/agent/tools.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-gitlisten-'));
+
+// A signal that tracks its 'abort' listeners so we can assert cleanup after a normal finish.
+const abortListeners = new Set();
+let peakListeners = 0;
+const signal = {
+  aborted: false,
+  addEventListener: (t, fn) => { if (t === 'abort') { abortListeners.add(fn); peakListeners = Math.max(peakListeners, abortListeners.size); } },
+  removeEventListener: (t, fn) => { if (t === 'abort') abortListeners.delete(fn); },
+};
+
+try {
+  // A fast-failing URL (connection refused) — the clone finishes via close/error WITHOUT an abort.
+  const out = extractContent(await execute('git_clone', { url: 'https://127.0.0.1:1/repo.git' }, {}, { cwd: dir, signal, getGitLabCredentials: () => ({ user: 't', token: 't' }) }));
+  ok(/ERROR|fail|fatal|refused|clone/i.test(out), `the clone finished (with an error, as expected for a dead host): "${out.slice(0, 60)}…"`);
+  ok(peakListeners >= 1, 'git_clone DID register an abort listener while running (guards against a vacuous test)');
+  ok(abortListeners.size === 0, `git_clone removed its abort listener after finishing — no leak on the run signal (remaining: ${abortListeners.size})`);
+} finally {
+  await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+}
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-git-clone-tool.mjs
new a5d54077fcf0d120c86f47cf
+/**
+ * git_clone tool + the <js> git() runner's clone refusal:
+ *  [1] buildAuthedCloneUrl injects the configured user:token (URL-encoded), strips any creds the
+ *      caller embedded, and is a no-op when no token is configured.
+ *  [2] the git_clone tool rejects a non-HTTPS URL.
+ *  [3] git_clone never leaks the token in its output (redaction) — a failing clone returns an ERROR
+ *      with the token scrubbed.
+ *  [4] git(["clone", …]) inside <js> is REFUSED, pointing the agent at clone() (the same credential-injecting
+ *      clone as the git_clone tool).
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-git-clone-tool.mjs   (needs git on PATH)
+ */
+import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { mkdtemp, readdir, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { tools, buildAuthedCloneUrl, execute, extractContent } from '../src/agent/tools.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+function haveGit() { try { execFileSync('git', ['--version'], { stdio: 'ignore' }); return true; } catch { return false; } }
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\n[1] buildAuthedCloneUrl: inject / strip / encode / no-op');
+  const creds = { user: 'svc@corp', token: 'glpat-aB3/x:9#z' };
+  const u1 = buildAuthedCloneUrl('https://gitlab.example.mil/group/repo.git', creds);
+  ok(u1 === 'https://svc%40corp:glpat-aB3%2Fx%3A9%23z@gitlab.example.mil/group/repo.git', `plain URL gets URL-encoded creds (got ${u1})`);
+  ok((() => { try { const p = new URL(u1); return decodeURIComponent(p.password) === creds.token && p.host === 'gitlab.example.mil'; } catch { return false; } })(), 'the result is a valid URL whose password round-trips to the token');
+  const u2 = buildAuthedCloneUrl('https://attacker:leaked@gitlab.example.mil/group/repo.git', creds);
+  ok(u2 === 'https://svc%40corp:glpat-aB3%2Fx%3A9%23z@gitlab.example.mil/group/repo.git' && !u2.includes('attacker') && !u2.includes('leaked'), 'creds embedded by the caller are STRIPPED and replaced with the configured ones');
+  ok(buildAuthedCloneUrl('https://host/x.git', null) === 'https://host/x.git', 'no configured creds → URL unchanged');
+  ok(buildAuthedCloneUrl('https://host/x.git', { user: 'u', token: '' }) === 'https://host/x.git', 'empty token → URL unchanged');
+
+  console.log('\n[2] git_clone rejects a non-HTTPS URL');
+  let threw = false;
+  try { await tools.git_clone.impl({ url: 'git@gitlab.example.mil:group/repo.git' }, { getGitLabCredentials: () => creds }); } catch (e) { threw = /http\(s\) URL/i.test(e.message); }
+  ok(threw, 'an SSH/scp-style URL is refused with an http(s)-only message');
+
+  if (!haveGit()) { console.log('\nSKIP [3]: git not found'); }
+  else {
+    console.log('\n[3] git_clone never leaks the token in its output (failing clone → redacted ERROR)');
+    // Unreachable host → the clone fails fast; assert the token is NOT present in the returned text.
+    const res = await tools.git_clone.impl(
+      { url: 'https://nonexistent.invalid.localhost.test/group/repo.git' },
+      { getGitLabCredentials: () => creds, cwd: process.cwd() },
+    );
+    ok(typeof res === 'string' && res.startsWith('ERROR:'), 'a failing clone returns an ERROR');
+    ok(!res.includes(creds.token) && !res.includes(encodeURIComponent(creds.token)), 'the token (raw AND URL-encoded) is redacted from the output');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[4] the git() function REFUSES a raw `git clone` and points at clone()');
+  // (The shell that once carried this screen is gone; git(["clone", …]) inside a snippet is the remaining way
+  // to ask for one without credentials, and it is refused the same way — a commit message that merely mentions
+  // "git clone" is an argument, not the subcommand, so it is not.)
+  {
+    const { mkdtemp, rm } = await import('node:fs/promises');
+    const path = await import('node:path');
+    const { tmpdir } = await import('node:os');
+    const { execute, extractContent } = await import('../src/agent/tools.mjs');
+    const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-clone4-'));
+    const ctx = { cwd: dir, projectDir: dir };
+    const r = extractContent(await execute('js_eval', { code: 'try { await git(["clone", "https://gitlab.example.mil/group/repo.git"]); return "RAN"; } catch (e) { return "REFUSED: " + e.message; }' }, {}, ctx));
+    ok(/^REFUSED/.test(r) && /clone\(/.test(r), `git(["clone", …]) is refused → clone() (got "${String(r).slice(0, 70)}")`);
+    const b = extractContent(await execute('js_eval', { code: 'try { const x = await git(["log", "-1", "--format=%s", "--grep", "git clone"], { allowFail: true }); return "RAN " + x.code; } catch (e) { return "REFUSED: " + e.message; }' }, {}, ctx));
+    ok(/^RAN/.test(b), `an argument that merely mentions "git clone" is NOT refused (got "${String(b).slice(0, 50)}")`);
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * Commit-message context is cut EXACTLY at the repo's last commit (not just "recent messages"). A message sent
+ * BEFORE the last commit must NOT appear in the commit-rec prompt; a message sent AFTER it must. Verified by
+ * capturing the prompt the (mock) adapter receives. Also checks lastCommitTimeMs.
+ * Run: node test/test-git-commit-exact-cut.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { execSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-cut-home-'));   // its own home: never the developer's ~/.code_boss
+const { lastCommitTimeMs } = await import('../src/agent/git-commit.mjs');
+process.env.CB_GIT_SCAN_MS = '100000';   // don't let the timer fire the rec; we trigger it via the endpoint
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+let capturedRecPrompt = null;
+const adapter = {
+  async complete({ messages }) {
+    const text = (messages || []).map((m) => m.content).join('\n');
+    if (/Reply with ONLY a JSON array|Group the units into commits/.test(text)) {
+      capturedRecPrompt = text;
+      const ids = [...new Set([...text.matchAll(/\[([^\]\s]+)\]/g)].map((m) => m[1]).filter((id) => /#\d+$/.test(id) || /\.[a-z0-9]+$/i.test(id)))];
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: JSON.stringify([{ message: 'wip', units: ids }]) } }] };
+    }
+    return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] };
+  },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-cut-'));
+  execSync('git init -q', { cwd: proj });
+  execSync('git config user.email t@t && git config user.name t && git config commit.gpgsign false', { cwd: proj });
+  await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'base.txt'), 'base\n');
+  execSync('git add -A && git commit -qm base', { cwd: proj });
+
+  const PORT = 29615, BASE = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+  const send = (text) => fetch(BASE + '/api/message', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, model: 'm', critic: false, phaseAttack: false }) });
+  const waitIdle = async () => { for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) { const s = (await (await fetch(BASE + '/api/state')).json()).status; if (s === 'idle' || s === 'done') return; await sleep(50); } };
+  try {
+    ok(Math.abs(lastCommitTimeMs(proj) - Date.now()) < 20000, 'lastCommitTimeMs returns the base commit time (~now)');
+
+    await send('walnut this is before the commit'); await waitIdle();   // a message BEFORE the cut commit
+    await sleep(1200);   // git %ct is second-resolution — ensure a distinct second
+    await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'mid.txt'), 'mid\n');
+    execSync('git add -A && git commit -qm "mid commit"', { cwd: proj });   // THE cut point
+    await sleep(1200);
+    await send('pepper this is after the commit'); await waitIdle();      // a message AFTER the cut
+
+    await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'work.txt'), 'dev change\n');           // an uncommitted change to recommend
+    // Trigger the rec computation + wait for it to run (captures the prompt).
+    let recs; for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { recs = await (await fetch(BASE + '/api/git-commit-recs?path=' + encodeURIComponent(proj))).json(); if (recs.status === 'ready' || capturedRecPrompt) break; await sleep(150); }
+    ok(!!capturedRecPrompt, 'the commit-rec prompt was built');
+    ok(capturedRecPrompt && /pepper/.test(capturedRecPrompt), 'the AFTER-commit message IS in the prompt');
+    ok(capturedRecPrompt && !/walnut/.test(capturedRecPrompt), 'the BEFORE-commit message is NOT in the prompt (exact cut)');
+  } finally { server.close(); await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

new e8a8fc1bfebdc7c2a3ca8ded
+/**
+ * COMMIT SUGGESTIONS AFTER EACH RUN, AND WHERE ONE IS STUCK (the developer, 2026-10-30: "it's been running for 15min, so
+ * maybe it's just hanging ... Can the inspector see the commit suggestion attempts and troubleshoot? ... give some more
+ * information on the commit suggestion loading so I can see where it's getting stuck"; "instead of each minute, let's do
+ * it immediately after each run that made changes"; "make the model for that selectable, but defaulting to the main
+ * agent's model"; and the modal: "an intermediate page if it's still loading that shows the diff and then a loading
+ * animation and button always visible at the top"):
+ *   [1] a model call that never answers ends at its time limit: a failure, the repo not left busy
+ *   [2] a run that changes files: no suggestion while it runs, one right when it ends - no click; on the model chosen
+ *       in Settings (the main one when none is)
+ *   [3] the Inspector's look tool="commits": every attempt, why, and each stage's time
+ *   [4] the modal while one is made: all the changes, the stage and its time, the way to the suggestions enabled when
+ *       they are ready
+ * Run: node test/test-git-commit-live.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { execSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-gclive-home-'));
+process.env.CB_GIT_SCAN_MS = '100000';
+process.env.CB_COMMIT_RECS_RETRY_MS = '600000';   // no background retry of a failure in this test
+process.env.CB_COMMIT_RECS_CALL_MS = '6000';

+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const C = '/--- you ab12 ---';
+
+let hang = 0, slowMs = 200, running = false;
+const recCalls = [];   // { model, at, duringRun }
+const isRecPrompt = (text) => /Reply with ONLY a JSON array|Group the units into commits/.test(text);
+const recReply = (text) => {
+  const ids = [...new Set([...text.matchAll(/\[([^\]\s]+)\]/g)].map((m) => m[1]).filter((id) => /#\d+$/.test(id) || /\.[a-z0-9]+$/i.test(id)))];
+  return JSON.stringify([{ message: 'feat: the changes', units: ids }]);
+};
+const inspectorSaw = [];
+const adapter = {
+  async complete({ model, messages, signal }) {
+    const text = (messages || []).map((m) => m.content).join('\n');
+    if (isRecPrompt(text)) {
+      recCalls.push({ model, at: Date.now(), duringRun: running });
+      if (hang > 0) { hang--; await new Promise((_, rej) => { if (signal) signal.addEventListener('abort', () => rej(new Error('aborted')), { once: true }); }); }
+      await sleep(slowMs);
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: recReply(text) } }] };
+    }
+    if (/INSPECTOR/.test(text)) {
+      inspectorSaw.push(text);
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: inspectorSaw.length % 2 ? '<look tool="commits"/>' : 'Looked.' } }] };
+    }
+    return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] };
+  },
+  // the run: two writes in two steps, then a while before it ends (the scans the writes ask for come mid-run)
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const all = (req.messages || []).map((x) => (typeof x.content === 'string' ? x.content : JSON.stringify(x.content))).join('\n');
+    const after = all.slice(all.lastIndexOf('WRITE-TWO'));
+    const done = (after.match(/What the snippet did/g) || []).length;
+    running = true;
+    let body;
+    if (done === 0) body = 'Writing. <remote-workspace><js>await write("run-one.js", "module.exports = 1;\\n"); return "ok";</js></remote-workspace>';
+    else if (done === 1) { await sleep(1200); body = 'More. <remote-workspace><js>await write("run-two.js", "module.exports = 2;\\n"); return "ok";</js></remote-workspace>'; }
+    else { await sleep(2500); body = 'Done.\n' + C; }
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: body } }] };
+  },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }, { id: 'q' }]; },
+};
+
+const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-gclive-'));

+/**
+ * COMMIT SUGGESTIONS ARE READY BEFORE THE CLICK (the developer, 2026-10-15: "It's never worked well where it's supposed
+ * to pregenerate commit messages when it starts up. Every time I click on a branch that has a number bubble, it calls
+ * the LLM and takes forever.") - counts the model calls a click makes, in each way a click used to wait for one:
+ *   [1] at startup, a dirty repo's suggestions are made once; clicking (twice) makes no call
+ *   [2] a suggestion that failed is not made again by every click - it says so, and the background tries once more
+ *   [3] the agent changes files during a run: the suggestions are made when the run ends, not at the click
+ *   [4] after a restart with the same changes, the suggestions kept on disk are used - no call
+ *   [5] a repo whose git status could not be read says so ("?"), not an empty bubble (ui.html, checked by source)
+ * Run: node test/test-git-commit-pregen.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, writeFile, readFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { execSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-gcpre-home-'));
+process.env.CB_GIT_SCAN_MS = '100000';           // no periodic scan: every call counted here has a cause the test made
+process.env.CB_COMMIT_RECS_RETRY_MS = '1500';    // the one background retry after a failure, soon
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const C = '/--- you ab12 ---';
+
+let recCalls = 0, failNext = 0;
+const isRecPrompt = (text) => /Reply with ONLY a JSON array|Group the units into commits/.test(text);
+const recReply = (text) => {
+  const ids = [...new Set([...text.matchAll(/\[([^\]\s]+)\]/g)].map((m) => m[1]).filter((id) => /#\d+$/.test(id) || /\.[a-z0-9]+$/i.test(id)))];
+  return JSON.stringify([{ message: 'feat: the changes', units: ids }]);
+};
+// the worker's turn: write one file in a snippet, then finish
+let workerStep = 0;
+const adapter = {
+  async complete({ messages }) {
+    const text = (messages || []).map((m) => m.content).join('\n');
+    if (isRecPrompt(text)) {
+      recCalls++;
+      await sleep(300);
+      if (failNext > 0) { failNext--; throw new Error('gateway timeout (test)'); }
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: recReply(text) } }] };
+    }
+    return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] };
+  },
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const all = (req.messages || []).map((x) => typeof x.content === 'string' ? x.content : JSON.stringify(x.content)).join('\n');
+    const body = !/What the snippet did/.test(all.slice(all.lastIndexOf('WRITE-ONE'))) ? 'Writing. <remote-workspace><js>await write("from-run.js", "module.exports = 2;\\n"); return "ok";</js></remote-workspace>' : 'Done.\n' + C;
+    workerStep++;
+    // the run goes on after its last write, as a real one does (it reports, it checks): the scan the write asks for
+    // comes while the run is still going
+    if (/^Done/.test(body)) await sleep(2500);
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: body } }] };
+  },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
