+      // icons: written byte-for-byte from the embedded base64
+      lcWriteIcons(c);
+      const appDir = path.join(home, '.code_boss', 'app');
+      ok(statSync(path.join(appDir, 'orange-star.ico')).size === statSync(path.join(ROOT, 'deploy', 'orange-star.ico')).size, 'orange-star.ico written at the source byte size');
+      ok(statSync(path.join(appDir, 'stop-icon.ico')).size === statSync(path.join(ROOT, 'deploy', 'stop-icon.ico')).size, 'stop-icon.ico written at the source byte size');
+      // an installed star that is exactly an older one of ours is replaced; anything else is kept (2026-10-26, Copper)
+      {
+        const { LAUNCHER_ICON_STAR_B64, LAUNCHER_ICON_STAR_OLD_SHA256S } = await import('../src/launcher-icons.mjs');
+        const { createHash } = await import('node:crypto');
+        const { execFileSync } = await import('node:child_process');
+        const star = path.join(appDir, 'orange-star.ico');
+        const sha = (b) => createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');
+        // the star as shipped before the recolour, from git (the commit before scripts/make-brand-star-icon.mjs ran)
+        let oldStar = null;
+        try {
+          const rev = execFileSync('git', ['log', '--format=%H', '-n', '1', '--', 'deploy/orange-star.ico'], { cwd: ROOT, encoding: 'utf8' }).trim();
+          for (const at of [`${rev}~1`, rev]) {
+            try { const blob = execFileSync('git', ['show', `${at}:deploy/orange-star.ico`], { cwd: ROOT, maxBuffer: 1 << 24 }); if (LAUNCHER_ICON_STAR_OLD_SHA256S.includes(sha(blob))) { oldStar = blob; break; } } catch {}
+          }
+        } catch {}
+        ok(oldStar, 'the old orange star is found in git, and is one the launcher knows');
+        ok(LAUNCHER_ICON_STAR_OLD_SHA256S.length >= 1 && !LAUNCHER_ICON_STAR_OLD_SHA256S.includes(sha(Buffer.from(LAUNCHER_ICON_STAR_B64, 'base64'))), 'the old stars are listed, and the current one is not among them');
+        if (oldStar) {
+          writeFileSync(star, oldStar);
+          lcWriteIcons(c);
+          ok(sha(readFileSync(star)) === sha(Buffer.from(LAUNCHER_ICON_STAR_B64, 'base64')), 'an installed old orange star is replaced with the current one');
+        }
+        writeFileSync(star, Buffer.from('a custom icon of the developer\'s'));
+        lcWriteIcons(c);
+        ok(readFileSync(star, 'utf8') === 'a custom icon of the developer\'s', 'an icon that is not one of ours is kept');
+      }
+    } finally { await cleanupDir(home); await cleanupDir(src); }
+  }
+  {
+    // missing source AND no installed app → fails cleanly, no partial install
+    const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-lc-nosrc-'));
+    try {
+      const c = ctx(home, path.join(home, 'nope.mjs'));
+      const r = lcFirstRunInstall(c);
+      ok(!r.ok && !existsSync(path.join(home, '.code_boss', 'app', 'code-boss.mjs')), 'a missing source with no app fails cleanly (no partial install)');
+    } finally { await cleanupDir(home); }
+  }
+
+  // ── [D] kill scoping ──
+  console.log('\n[D] lcBuildKillPs keeps the name-narrowing + --kill exclusion');
+  {
+    const ps = lcBuildKillPs();
+    ok(/-Filter\s+"Name='node\.exe'\s+OR\s+Name='chrome\.exe'"/.test(ps), "narrows by process NAME (-Filter Name='node.exe' OR 'chrome.exe')");
+    ok(/code-boss\.mjs/.test(ps) && /\\\.code_boss\\data\\chrome/.test(ps), 'matches the app node + its chrome profiles');
+    ok(/-notlike\s+'\*--kill\*'/.test(ps), 'excludes the stopper itself (--kill)');
+    ok(/Write-Output\s+\$n/.test(ps), 'returns the terminated count');
+  }
+
+  // ── [E] THE FIX: teardown watcher never loses a one-shot exit ──
+  console.log('\n[E] lcWatchChildClose resolves across a blocking gap + when already exited');
+  {
+    // child exits at ~100ms; watcher attached IMMEDIATELY; a BLOCKING spawnSync gap spans the exit (reproduces the
+    // original hang: a one-shot 'exit' fired during blocking work would be lost if the listener were attached later).
+    const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['-e', 'setTimeout(()=>process.exit(0),100)']);
+    const watch = lcWatchChildClose(child);
+    spawnSync(process.execPath, ['-e', 'setTimeout(()=>{},500)']);   // block ~500ms; the child exits during it
+    const resolved = await Promise.race([watch.promise.then(() => true), sleep(3000).then(() => false)]);
+    ok(resolved === true, 'watcher resolves even though the child exited during a blocking gap (no lost exit → no hang)');
+  }
+  {
+    // already-exited child → resolve immediately (exitCode short-circuit)
+    const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['-e', 'process.exit(0)']);
+    await new Promise((r) => child.once('exit', r));
+    const watch = lcWatchChildClose(child);
+    const resolved = await Promise.race([watch.promise.then(() => true), sleep(2000).then(() => false)]);
+    ok(resolved === true, 'watcher resolves immediately when the child already exited before it was called');
+  }
+  {
+    // spawn failure → resolves via error (does not crash), state.error set
+    const bad = spawn(path.join(tmpdir(), 'definitely-not-a-real-binary-xyz.exe'), []);
+    const watch = lcWatchChildClose(bad);
+    const resolved = await Promise.race([watch.promise.then(() => true), sleep(2000).then(() => false)]);
+    ok(resolved === true && watch.state.error, 'a spawn error resolves the watcher (no uncaught crash) and records the error');
+  }
+
+  // ── [F] bundle dispatch (if built): BARE run is the launcher (the flip), and `launch` still works as an alias ──
+  console.log('\n[F] bare `node code-boss.mjs` is the launcher (flip); `launch` alias still works');
+  {
+    const bundle = path.join(ROOT, 'dist', 'code-boss.mjs');
+    if (!existsSync(bundle)) { console.log('  -- skipped (no dist/code-boss.mjs; run node scripts/build.mjs first)'); }
+    else {
+      // (code_boss runs only in its container; this test runs the launcher on purpose outside one - the override)
+      const runBundle = (args, home, outside = true) => new Promise((resolve) => {
+        const env = { ...process.env, CB_HOME: home };
+        if (outside) env.CB_ALLOW_OUTSIDE_CONTAINER = '1'; else { delete env.CB_ALLOW_OUTSIDE_CONTAINER; delete env.CB_IN_CONTAINER; }
+        const c = spawn(process.execPath, [bundle, ...args], { env });
+        let out = ''; c.stdout.on('data', (d) => { out += d; }); c.stderr.on('data', (d) => { out += d; });
+        c.on('close', (code) => resolve({ out, code }));
+      });
+      const h0 = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-lc-refused-'));
+      const refused = await runBundle(['--writeloading'], h0, false);
+      ok(refused.code === 1 && /runs only inside its container/.test(refused.out) && !existsSync(path.join(h0, '.code_boss', 'loading.html')), `outside a container the app refuses to run, and says where to start it (exit ${refused.code})`);
+      const h1 = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-lc-bare-'));
+      const h2 = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-lc-alias-'));
+      try {
+        const bare = await runBundle(['--writeloading'], h1);   // NO subcommand → launcher → --writeloading
+        ok(bare.code === 0 && existsSync(path.join(h1, '.code_boss', 'loading.html')), `bare \`code-boss.mjs --writeloading\` runs the launcher (exit ${bare.code})`);

+        ok(alias.code === 0 && /launcher plan/.test(alias.out) && /server cmd = .*code-boss\.mjs" serve/.test(alias.out), 'the `launch` alias still works, and the server cmd it plans is `… serve`');
+      } finally { await cleanupDir(h1); await cleanupDir(h2); }
+    }
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * THE LAUNCHER'S WINDOW CLOSED: THE SERVER IS ASKED TO STOP (src/launch-cmd.mjs lcStopServer; the developer, 2026-11-03 -
+ * it was killed at once, so the last moment of the conversation could be lost).
+ *   [1] a server that stops when asked: 'stopped', nothing killed
+ *   [2] one that does not answer the ask: ended after the wait - 'killed'
+ *   [3] one that had stopped already (the page said it was closing): 'gone'
+ * Run: node test/test-launch-stop.mjs
+ */
+import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+import { mkdtemp } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-launch-stop-home-'));
+const { lcStopServer } = await import('../src/launch-cmd.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+// a stand-in server: answers /api/shutdown and exits - or, `deaf`, answers and stays
+const server = (port, deaf) => {
+  const src = `require('http').createServer((q, s) => { s.end('{}'); if (q.url === '/api/shutdown' && !${deaf}) setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), 100); }).listen(${port}, '127.0.0.1', () => console.log('up'));`;
+  const c = spawn(process.execPath, ['-e', src], { stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'ignore'] });
+  return new Promise((r) => c.stdout.once('data', () => r(c)));
+};
+const killed = [];
+const kill = (pid) => { killed.push(pid); try { process.kill(pid); } catch {} };
+
+console.log('\n[1] stops when asked');
+{
+  const c = await server(29741, false);
+  const how = await lcStopServer(29741, c.pid, { waitMs: 3000, kill });
+  ok(how === 'stopped' && !killed.includes(c.pid), `'${how}', nothing killed`);
+}
+console.log('\n[2] does not stop');
+{
+  const c = await server(29742, true);
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  const how = await lcStopServer(29742, c.pid, { waitMs: 1500, kill });
+  ok(how === 'killed' && killed.includes(c.pid) && Date.now() - t0 >= 1400, `'${how}' after the wait (${Date.now() - t0} ms)`);
+}
+console.log('\n[3] stopped already');
+{

+  c.kill(); await sleep(400);
+  const how = await lcStopServer(29743, c.pid, { waitMs: 1500, kill });
+  ok(how === 'gone', `'${how}'`);
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

=== add test/test-launch-update-check.mjs
new 5d31a49fc5183d64c10cc605
+/**
+ * A LAUNCH MUST RUN THE BUILD YOU PUBLISHED.
+ *
+ * Reported from production: "I build with --prod, users restart, nobody gets the new version — but one of them
+ * ran `node ./code-boss.mjs check-update` by hand and it worked." Two separate defects, both in the launcher.
+ *
+ *   1. lcRunUpdateCheck() sat inside `if (!reuse)`, so it ran only when the launcher STARTED a server. Closing
+ *      the Chrome window does not stop the server — the launcher says so itself ("a reused instance is left
+ *      running") and only kills a server it started — so every later launch reused it and never checked again.
+ *      Running the command by hand worked because it had become the only caller.
+ *   2. Even on a cold launch the check ran AFTER the server was up, so a release arrived on the launch after the
+ *      one that fetched it: permanently one build behind, which defeats a moving production tag. Its stated
+ *      reason was the loading page's 30-second readiness budget. That budget no longer exists.
+ *
+ * These are ordering and nesting invariants, so they are tested as such — against the source. A behavioural test
+ * would need Chrome, a detached server and a live GitLab, and still would not catch a brace or a swapped pair of
+ * lines, which is what actually broke both times.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-launch-update-check.mjs
+ */
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const SRC = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', 'src', 'launch-cmd.mjs');
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+const src = await readFile(SRC, 'utf8');
+const lines = src.split(/\r?\n/);
+const lineOf = (re) => lines.findIndex((l) => re.test(l));
+
+console.log('\n[1] the cold path updates BEFORE it starts the server');
+{
+  const install = lineOf(/const inst = lcFirstRunInstall\(ctx\);/);
+  const update = lineOf(/await lcUpdateBeforeStart\(ctx\);/);
+  const start = lineOf(/serverPid = lcStartServer\(ctx, chosenPort\);/);
+  ok(install > 0 && update > install, `the update runs after the install (${install + 1} -> ${update + 1})`);
+  ok(start > update, `and the server starts only after the update (${update + 1} -> ${start + 1})`);
+  // Getting this backwards is the whole bug, and it reads as a harmless reordering.
+  ok(update < start, 'so THIS launch runs the new build, not the launch after it');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] a hung network cannot hang the launch');
+{
+  ok(/LC_UPDATE_BUDGET_MS/.test(src), 'the pre-start update is bounded');
+  ok(/taking longer than/.test(src), 'and says so in the boot feed rather than silently giving up');
+  ok(/c\.unref\(\)/.test(src), 'a slow download is left to finish for next launch, not killed part-done');
+  ok(/CB_LAUNCH_UPDATE_MS/.test(src), 'and the budget is overridable, since nobody here knows the target network');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3] the background check still covers the reuse path, and only that');
+{
+  const bg = lineOf(/if \(reuse\) lcRunUpdateCheck\(ctx\);/);
+  ok(bg > 0, `the background check is scoped to reuse (line ${bg + 1})`);
+  ok(lines.filter((l) => /lcRunUpdateCheck\(ctx\);/.test(l)).length === 1, 'exactly one call site — a second would double-check every cold launch');
+  // The nesting defect: it must not sit back inside the branch that skips it on every reuse.
+  const reuseIdx = lineOf(/^\s*if \(!reuse\) \{/);
+  const depthAt = (idx) => {
+    let d = 0;
+    for (let i = 0; i <= idx; i++) {
+      const l = lines[i].replace(/\/\/.*$/, '').replace(/'[^']*'/g, "''").replace(/"[^"]*"/g, '""').replace(/`[^`]*`/g, '``');
+      for (const ch of l) { if (ch === '{') d++; else if (ch === '}') d--; }
+    }
+    return d;
+  };
+  ok(depthAt(bg) < depthAt(reuseIdx), `and sits shallower than the reuse body (${depthAt(bg)} < ${depthAt(reuseIdx)})`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[4] the loading page has no clock of its own — which is what permits a slow update');
+{
+  const page = src.slice(src.indexOf('var lastRender'), src.indexOf('setInterval(pullLog'));
+  ok(page.length > 200, 'found the embedded loading page');
+  ok(/b\.ready/.test(page), 'it navigates when the launcher publishes ready, however long that takes');
+  ok(!/give ?up|timed? ?out|failed after/i.test(page), 'and never gives up on a timer of its own');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[5] closing the window leaves the server running — the premise of the reuse defect');
+{
+  ok(/reused instance is left running/.test(src), 'a reused instance is still left running when the window closes');
+  ok(/if \(serverPid\) lcKillTree\(serverPid\)/.test(src), 'and only a server the launcher started is killed');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[6] the reasoning is written where the next person will look');
+{
+  const near = lines.slice(Math.max(0, lineOf(/await lcUpdateBeforeStart\(ctx\);/) - 6), lineOf(/await lcUpdateBeforeStart\(ctx\);/)).join('\n');
+  ok(/before the server starts/i.test(near), 'the comment says why the order matters');
+  ok(/30s|budget|timeout/i.test(src.slice(src.indexOf('async function lcUpdateBeforeStart') - 1200, src.indexOf('async function lcUpdateBeforeStart'))),
+    'and the old constraint is recorded as no longer applying, so nobody restores the old order');
+}
+
+console.log('\n' + pass + ' passed, ' + fail + ' failed');
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+
=== add test/test-live-model-default.mjs
new ded22e662995b77ea2c27d20
+/**
+ * Guard: no live probe carries its own model default.
+ *
+ * Twenty-five files each held `process.env.CB_MODEL || 'claude-haiku-4-5'`. Changing which model the live suites
+ * run against was therefore a twenty-five-file edit, and the failure mode of a duplicated default is silent: miss
+ * one file and it keeps running the old model while the report says otherwise, so a result gets attributed to the
+ * wrong model. That is expensive to notice and easy to prevent — the default lives in test/_live-model.mjs, and
+ * this file fails if a copy of it grows back anywhere else.
+ *
+ * A probe that genuinely wants a specific model still can: pin it explicitly rather than as a `CB_MODEL ||` default.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-live-model-default.mjs
+ */
+import { readdir, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

+/**
+ * LIVE OUTPUT, BY WHO WROTE IT — through the real UI, with a real snippet running real processes.
+ *
+ * A running snippet's live output was one stream: the script's console.log lines and every process's output mixed
+ * together, with nothing to say which was which. Now the script's lines are plain text and each process gets its
+ * own box, headed with the call, set apart, scrolling once it is long, collapsible.
+ *
+ *   [1] script lines, then a process's box (headed "node(slow.js)") with ITS lines, then the script's next line
+ *   [2] two processes running at once each keep their own box — no line lands in the other's
+ *   [3] a long process output scrolls inside its box and follows the end
+ *   [4] clicking a box's header collapses just that box; the view stays open
+ * Run: node test/test-live-output-procs-e2e.mjs
+ */

+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29595;

+    ok(!/NORTH_SAYS|SOUTH_SAYS/.test(br.loose), 'none of them among the script\'s own lines');

=== add test/test-llm-idle-timeout.mjs
new 769764fab25b7ff9f0e57bc2
+/**
+ * Streaming-adapter IDLE guard: if a stream stalls (no bytes for CB_LLM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS), the read
+ * aborts and completeStream throws (IdleTimeoutError) instead of hanging until the whole-chat
+ * timeout — so a hung leaf/proxy fails fast (and a workflow leaf degrades to null). A healthy
+ * stream that keeps emitting completes normally; idleMs<=0 disables the guard.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-llm-idle-timeout.mjs
+ */
+process.env.CB_LLM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS = '300';   // short window for the test (read per-stream)
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const enc = new TextEncoder();
+
+function resp(body) { return { ok: true, status: 200, statusText: 'OK', headers: { get: () => 'text/event-stream' }, body, text: async () => '' }; }
+const hungBody = () => ({ getReader: () => ({ read: () => new Promise(() => {}), cancel: async () => {} }) });   // read never resolves
+function sseBody(lines) {
+  let i = 0;
+  return { getReader: () => ({ read: async () => (i < lines.length ? { done: false, value: enc.encode(lines[i++]) } : { done: true }), cancel: async () => {} }) };
+}
+const drain = async (gen) => { const out = []; for await (const c of gen) { const t = c?.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content; if (t) out.push(t); } return out.join(''); };
+
+async function main() {
+  // The adapter's idle timer is unref'd (production-correct: it must not keep the process alive on
+  // its own). In this bare test nothing else holds the event loop, so keep it alive explicitly or
+  // node would exit before the timer fires.
+  const keepalive = setInterval(() => {}, 5000);
+  console.log('\n[1] a stalled stream throws an idle timeout (does NOT hang)');
+  {
+    const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: 'http://x/v1', apiKey: 'k', fetchImpl: async () => resp(hungBody()) });
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    let err = null;
+    try { await drain(adapter.completeStream({ model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }] })); }
+    catch (e) { err = e; }
+    const dt = Date.now() - t0;
+    ok(err && /idle/i.test(err.message || ''), `threw an idle error (got ${err?.name}: ${err?.message?.slice(0, 40)})`);
+    ok(dt < 1500, `failed fast (~${dt}ms, near the 300ms window — not hanging)`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[2] a healthy stream completes normally (no false idle trip)');
+  {
+    const lines = [
+      'data: {"type":"message_start","message":{"usage":{"input_tokens":5}}}\n',
+      'data: {"type":"content_block_delta","delta":{"type":"text_delta","text":"hello "}}\n',
+      'data: {"type":"content_block_delta","delta":{"type":"text_delta","text":"world"}}\n',
+      'data: {"type":"message_stop"}\n',
+    ];
+    const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: 'http://x/v1', apiKey: 'k', fetchImpl: async () => resp(sseBody(lines)) });
+    let text = null, err = null;
+    try { text = await drain(adapter.completeStream({ model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }] })); }
+    catch (e) { err = e; }
+    ok(!err, `no error on a healthy stream (got ${err?.message || 'none'})`);
+    ok(text === 'hello world', `streamed the full content (got "${text}")`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3] idleMs<=0 disables the guard (a healthy stream still completes)');
+  {
+    process.env.CB_LLM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS = '0';
+    const lines = ['data: {"type":"content_block_delta","delta":{"type":"text_delta","text":"ok"}}\n', 'data: {"type":"message_stop"}\n'];
+    const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: 'http://x/v1', apiKey: 'k', fetchImpl: async () => resp(sseBody(lines)) });
+    let text = null, err = null;
+    try { text = await drain(adapter.completeStream({ model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }] })); }
+    catch (e) { err = e; }
+    ok(!err && text === 'ok', `disabled guard still streams normally (got "${text}"${err ? ', err ' + err.message : ''})`);
+    process.env.CB_LLM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS = '300';
+  }
+
+  clearInterval(keepalive);
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });
+
=== add test/test-llm-retry.mjs
new a6356f5993977fdf22262e13
+// test-llm-retry.mjs — a background call that timed out is tried again (the developer, 2026-10-26: "looks like the
+// bookkeeper timed out just now and it doesn't retry"); a stop and a model's answer are not
+import { isLlmTimeout, retryLlmOnTimeout, LLM_RETRY_WAITS_MS } from '../src/agent/llm-retry.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, m) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', m); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', m); } };
+const named = (name, msg = name) => Object.assign(new Error(msg), { name });
+const http = (status) => Object.assign(new Error(`HTTP ${status} x: {}`), { status });
+
+console.log('\n[1] what counts as a timeout');
+ok(isLlmTimeout(named('IdleTimeoutError', 'LLM stream idle — no data for 120000ms')), 'the stream gone silent');
+ok(isLlmTimeout(new Error('fetch failed')) && isLlmTimeout(Object.assign(new Error('fetch failed'), { cause: { code: 'ECONNRESET' } })), 'a dropped connection');
+ok(isLlmTimeout(http(504)) && isLlmTimeout(http(408)) && isLlmTimeout(http(524)), 'a gateway that gave up waiting: 504, 408, 524');
+ok(isLlmTimeout(new Error('could not reach the LLM endpoint https://x (ETIMEDOUT) after 4 attempts')), 'an endpoint not reached');
+ok(!isLlmTimeout(named('AbortError', 'aborted')), 'not a stop');
+const ac = new AbortController(); ac.abort();
+ok(!isLlmTimeout(named('IdleTimeoutError'), ac.signal), 'not when the run was stopped, whatever the error says');
+ok(!isLlmTimeout(http(400)) && !isLlmTimeout(http(401)) && !isLlmTimeout(http(500)), 'not another HTTP answer (400, 401; a 500 the adapter retried already)');
+ok(!isLlmTimeout(new Error('the bookkeeper brought no <timeline-ops>')), 'not an error of ours');
+ok(LLM_RETRY_WAITS_MS.length === 2, 'two more tries');
+
+console.log('\n[2] tried again');
+{
+  let calls = 0; const told = [];
+  const r = await retryLlmOnTimeout(async () => { calls++; if (calls === 1) throw named('IdleTimeoutError', 'LLM stream idle'); return 'the ops'; }, { waits: [5, 5], onRetry: (x) => told.push(x) });
+  ok(r === 'the ops' && calls === 2 && told.length === 1 && told[0].attempt === 1 && told[0].of === 2, 'a timeout, then the answer: two calls, one retry told');
+}
+{
+  let calls = 0;
+  let err = null;
+  try { await retryLlmOnTimeout(async () => { calls++; throw http(504); }, { waits: [5, 5] }); } catch (e) { err = e; }
+  ok(calls === 3 && err && err.status === 504, `three timeouts: three calls, then the error stands (${calls})`);
+}
+{
+  let calls = 0, err = null;
+  try { await retryLlmOnTimeout(async () => { calls++; throw http(400); }, { waits: [5, 5] }); } catch (e) { err = e; }
+  ok(calls === 1 && err.status === 400, 'an answer is not tried again');
+}
+{
+  const c = new AbortController();
+  let calls = 0, err = null;
+  const p = retryLlmOnTimeout(async () => { calls++; throw named('IdleTimeoutError'); }, { signal: c.signal, waits: [2000, 2000] });
+  setTimeout(() => c.abort(), 20);
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  try { await p; } catch (e) { err = e; }
+  ok(calls === 1 && err && err.name === 'AbortError' && Date.now() - t0 < 1000, 'stopped while waiting to try again: it stops at once, no more calls');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+
=== add test/test-llm-rows-toggle.mjs
new f2aca5ba42e5e68cda865131
+/**
+ * MODEL-CALL ROWS: RECORDED ALWAYS, SHOWN OPTIONALLY.
+ *
+ * Every model round trip appends a muted row plus its receipt — "llm(worker turn 3 · … · 15.3s)" / "⎿ 1552
+ * chars". One developer turn produces four to eight of them, so reading a real transcript means picking the
+ * agent's actual sentences out of a column of receipts. Driving a live session, that was the single most
+ * consistent thing in the way.
+ *
+ * Hidden by default now, with a Settings toggle. Nothing about the RECORDING changes — the raw log still has
+ * every byte and clicking a message still opens the exchange — so this is purely what the transcript renders.
+ *
+ * The thing worth guarding: `meta-row` also covers `nudge` and `retry`, which are the agent's visible
+ * self-correction and must stay on screen. Hiding those too would be an easy and invisible over-reach.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-llm-rows-toggle.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+const HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-llmrows-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29295;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const SEP = String.fromCharCode(92);
+const chrome = [

+      // worse than showing both.
+      const orphan = await page.evaluate(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('.row.tool.llm-row + .row-result')].filter((e) => e.offsetParent !== null).length);
+      ok(orphan === 0, 'and no orphaned receipt is left behind');
+      ok(await page.evaluate(() => document.body.classList.contains('hide-llm-rows')), 'the body carries the hiding class');
+    }

+ * THE LLM CANNOT BE REACHED: THE RUN WAITS FOR IT (the developer, 2026-11-02: "we shouldn't be giving up after four

+ * The manager page's spinners turn IN STEP (the developer, 2026-10-11): WebLogic's and code_boss's, started a moment
+ * apart, were out of step - a CSS animation starts when its class is set. The page now starts each at time 0 of its

+import path from 'node:path';

+      const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+      const lamp = () => { const s = document.createElement('span'); s.className = 'lamp'; document.body.appendChild(s); return s; };
+      const a = lamp(), b = lamp();
+      a.className = 'lamp busy';                         // WebLogic starts
+      await wait(400);
+      b.className = 'lamp busy';                         // code_boss, 400 ms later
+      const ol = document.createElement('ol'); ol.className = 'steps'; ol.innerHTML = '<li class="run"><span class="dot"></span></li>'; document.body.appendChild(ol);
+      await wait(200);
+      const at = (el) => { const x = el.getAnimations()[0]; return x ? Math.round(Number(x.currentTime) % 900) : null; };
+      return { a: at(a), b: at(b), dot: at(ol.querySelector('.dot')) };
+    });
+    ok(phases.a !== null && phases.b !== null && Math.abs(phases.a - phases.b) <= 1 && Math.abs(phases.a - phases.dot) <= 1,
+      `two lamps made busy 400 ms apart, and a step's dot, are at the same point of the turn: ${phases.a} / ${phases.b} / ${phases.dot} ms of 900`);
+  } finally { await browser.close(); }
+}
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

=== add test/test-mandatory-plugins.mjs
new deea070289691b55826f20bd
+/**
+ * A PLUGIN THE APP WILL NOT TALK TO A MODEL WITHOUT.
+ *
+ * Shipping a redaction plugin makes it present. It does not make it RUNNING, and this session found three ways it
+ * can be absent while everything looks fine: a build made from an extract that omitted its source installs
+ * nothing, a developer can switch it off in Settings, and a load error leaves it in the snapshot disabled. In all
+ * three the app carried on and every message went out in the clear.
+ *
+ * So the check happens where it cannot be routed around: the outbound adapter wrap, which every pass flows
+ * through — worker turns, reviewer forks, compaction, commit recommendations. If a mandatory plugin is not loaded
+ * and enabled, the call is refused and nothing is sent.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-mandatory-plugins.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-mandatory-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+const { MANDATORY_PLUGINS, missingMandatory, mandatoryError } = await import('../src/plugins/bundled.mjs');
+const { userPluginDisabledPath, userConfigDir } = await import('../src/plugins/paths.mjs');
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const PORT = Number(process.env.CB_MANDATORY_PORT || 29655);
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\n[A] which states count as missing');
+  {
+    ok(MANDATORY_PLUGINS.includes('piishape'), `piishape is mandatory (${JSON.stringify(MANDATORY_PLUGINS)})`);
+    ok(missingMandatory([{ name: 'piishape', enabled: true }]).length === 0, 'loaded and enabled is the healthy state');
+    ok(missingMandatory([{ name: 'piishape', enabled: false }]).join() === 'piishape', 'installed but switched OFF counts as missing');
+    ok(missingMandatory([]).join() === 'piishape', 'and absent entirely counts as missing');
+    ok(missingMandatory(null).join() === 'piishape', 'a snapshot that failed to build is not a pass');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[B] the refusal says what is wrong and both ways out');
+  {
+    const m = mandatoryError(['piishape']);
+    ok(/REFUSED/.test(m) && /Nothing was sent/.test(m), 'it says the call was refused and nothing went out');
+    ok(/Settings/.test(m), 'it points at the toggle, for the case where it is switched off');
+    ok(/plugins folder/.test(m), 'and at the folder, for the case where the build has no copy');
+    ok(/CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS=0/.test(m), 'and names the override rather than leaving someone stuck');
+    ok(!/undefined|\[object/.test(m), 'and reads as a sentence');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[C] the override lifts it, and only when set');
+  {
+    const before = process.env.CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS;
+    process.env.CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS = '0';
+    ok(missingMandatory([]).length === 0, 'CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS=0 lets the app run without it');
+    process.env.CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS = '1';
+    ok(missingMandatory([]).join() === 'piishape', 'any other value leaves the check on');
+    if (before === undefined) delete process.env.CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS; else process.env.CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS = before;
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[D] END TO END: with the plugin switched off, the model is never called');
+  {
+    // The developer turning it off in Settings is the most likely way this happens, and the one where the app
+    // would otherwise carry on looking entirely normal.
+    await mkdir(userConfigDir(), { recursive: true });
+    await writeFile(userPluginDisabledPath(), JSON.stringify(['piishape']), 'utf8');
+    const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-mandatory-proj-'));
+    await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'people.csv'), 'first_name,last_name,ssn\nRutherford,Havisham,482-11-9037\n', 'utf8');
+
+    let called = 0, sawText = '';
+    const adapter = {
+      baseURL: '(mock)',
+      async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+      async complete(req) {
+        called++;
+        sawText = JSON.stringify(req.messages || '');
+        return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'hi' } }] };
+      },
+    };
+    const { server } = await startServer({
+      html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+      defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj,
+    });
+    try {
+      await sleep(1500);   // past the first plugin reconcile
+      const state = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`)).json();
+      const row = (state.plugins || []).find((p) => p.name === 'piishape');
+      ok(row && row.enabled === false, `piishape is present but switched off (${row ? row.enabled : 'absent'})`);
+
+      await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/message`, {
+        method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
+        body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'read people.csv and tell me who is in it', model: 'm' }),
+      }).catch(() => {});
+      for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
+        const st = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`).then((r) => r.json()).then((j) => j.status).catch(() => null);
+        if (['done', 'idle', 'error'].includes(st)) break;
+        await sleep(250);
+      }
+      await sleep(500);
+
+      ok(called === 0, `the model was never called (${called} call(s))`);
+      ok(!/Havisham|482-11-9037/.test(sawText), 'so no value from the project could have reached it');
+      const st2 = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`)).json();
+      const blob = JSON.stringify(st2);
+      if (!/REFUSED/.test(blob)) console.log('    [state keys] ' + Object.keys(st2).join(', ') + ' | status=' + st2.status + ' | lastError=' + JSON.stringify(st2.lastError || st2.error || null).slice(0, 200));
+      ok(/REFUSED/.test(blob) && /piishape/.test(blob), 'and the refusal is on screen, naming the plugin');
+    } finally {
+      server.close();
+      await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+    }
+  }
+
+  await rm(HOME, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * A "> " QUOTE IS A QUOTE, in a finished reply and while it streams. Found live (2026-10-24): historyAsk answers by
+ * quoting the record word for word, and the reply showed every ">" as text, an empty quote line as a lone ">", and a
+ * code block inside the quote with "> " still on each of its lines, outside the quote.
+ * In a real browser against the served ui.html: renderInline (a finished reply) and createStreamingMd (one streaming).
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-markdown-quote.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+const HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-mdquote-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 28957;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const SEP = String.fromCharCode(92);
+const chrome = [
+  'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '').split(SEP).join('/') + '/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+// the reply as it came, trimmed: a quote with an empty line and a code block in it, then the answer
+const NL = String.fromCharCode(10), F = '```';
+const REPLY = [
+  'The reviewer flagged it as Issue 4:',
+  '> **invoiceTotal names "invoice undefined" when invoice.id is missing**',
+  '>',
+  '> ' + F,
+  '> invoiceTotal({ taxRate: 0 })',
+  '>   -> THREW: invoice undefined: lines is missing',
+  '> ' + F,
+  '>',
+  '> The test does not cover this case at all.',
+  '',
+  'The decision was to leave it alone.',
+].join(NL);
+
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-mdquote-'));
+const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+const { server } = await startServer({
+  html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+  defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; } },
+  modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: dir,
+});
+const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+try {
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => typeof window.renderInline === 'function' && typeof window.createStreamingMd === 'function', { timeout: 10000 });
+  // what a reader sees: the quote's text, its code, and what is outside it
+  const look = (root) => {
+    const q = root.querySelector('blockquote');
+    return {
+      quotes: root.querySelectorAll('blockquote').length,
+      inQuote: q ? q.innerText : '',
+      codeInQuote: q && q.querySelector('pre code') ? q.querySelector('pre code').textContent : null,
+      bold: !!(q && q.querySelector('strong')),
+      outside: [...root.children].filter((e) => e.tagName !== 'BLOCKQUOTE').map((e) => e.innerText).join(' | '),
+      text: root.innerText,
+    };
+  };
+  const checks = (label, r) => {
+    ok(r.quotes === 1, `${label}: one quote (${r.quotes})`);
+    ok(r.codeInQuote != null && /invoiceTotal\(\{ taxRate: 0 \}\)/.test(r.codeInQuote) && !/^>/m.test(r.codeInQuote), `${label}: the code block is inside the quote, without the "> " (${JSON.stringify(r.codeInQuote)})`);
+    ok(/The test does not cover this case/.test(r.inQuote) && r.bold, `${label}: the quote's text is in it, bold kept`);
+    ok(!/^\s*>/m.test(r.text) && !/&gt;/.test(r.text), `${label}: no ">" is left as text`);
+    ok(/Issue 4/.test(r.outside) && /leave it alone/.test(r.outside) && !/leave it alone/.test(r.inQuote), `${label}: the lines before and after are outside it`);
+  };
+
+  console.log('\n[1] a finished reply (renderInline)');
+  const fin = await page.evaluate((src, lookSrc) => { const d = document.createElement('div'); d.innerHTML = window.renderInline(src); document.body.appendChild(d); const r = (0, eval)('(' + lookSrc + ')')(d); d.remove(); return r; }, REPLY, look.toString());
+  checks('finished', fin);
+
+  console.log('\n[2] the same reply streaming in, a few characters at a time (createStreamingMd)');
+  const str = await page.evaluate(async (src, lookSrc) => {
+    const d = document.createElement('div'); document.body.appendChild(d);
+    const s = window.createStreamingMd(d);
+    const seen = [];
+    for (let i = 0; i < src.length; i += 3) { s.feed(src.slice(i, i + 3)); seen.push(d.innerText); }
+    s.finish();
+    const r = (0, eval)('(' + lookSrc + ')')(d);
+    r.flashes = seen.filter((t) => /^\s*>/m.test(t)).length;
+    d.remove();
+    return r;
+  }, REPLY, look.toString());
+  checks('streamed', str);
+  ok(str.flashes === 0, `while it streamed, no ">" ever showed as text (${str.flashes} moments)`);
+
+  console.log('\n[3] what is not a quote stays as it was');
+  const plain = await page.evaluate(() => window.renderInline('a -> b and x > 3\n' + '```' + '\n> inside a code block\n' + '```'));
+  ok(!/<blockquote/.test(plain) && /&gt; inside a code block/.test(plain) && /x &gt; 3/.test(plain), 'an arrow, a comparison and a ">" inside a code block are not quotes');
+  const two = await page.evaluate(() => window.renderInline('> first\n\nbetween\n\n> second'));
+  ok((two.match(/<blockquote/g) || []).length === 2 && /between/.test(two.replace(/<blockquote[\s\S]*?<\/blockquote>/g, '')), 'two quotes with text between are two quotes');
+} finally {
+  await browser.close();
+  server.close();
+  await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  await rm(HOME, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+}
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

+/**
+ * End-to-end (bug review #8): an UNBALANCED `**` in one paragraph must NOT bridge across the paragraph boundary
+ * into a later paragraph's `**` (which produced a `<strong>` spanning two .md-p divs + a stray `**`). The finalized
+ * render must have exactly one correct <strong>. Drives the real worker + UI. Run: node test/test-markdown-render-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29144;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+function chunkedAdapter(responses) {
+  let r = 0; const next = () => responses[Math.min(r++, responses.length - 1)];
+  return { async *completeStream() { for (const c of next()) { await sleep(4); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: c } }] }; } }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next().join('') } }] }; } };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-md-'));
+  // Paragraph 1 has an UNBALANCED ** (a stray bold marker); paragraph 2 has a BALANCED **critical**.
+  const responses = [
+    ['**Warning: do not delete these.\n\n', 'The **critical** files must stay.'],
+    [''],
+  ];
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const htmlWithConfig = html.replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: htmlWithConfig, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'test-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: chunkedAdapter(responses), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1100, height: 800 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(300);
+
+    console.log('\n[A] an unbalanced ** does not bridge across paragraphs (no corrupt cross-block <strong>)');
+    await page.type('#input', 'explain');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__sessionRef?.status === 'done' || window.__sessionRef?.status === 'idle', { timeout: 20000 });
+    await sleep(400);
+
+    const info = await page.$$eval('.row.assistant', (els) => {
+      const el = els[els.length - 1];
+      const body = el?.querySelector('.body');
+      const strongs = Array.from(body?.querySelectorAll('strong') || []).map((s) => s.textContent);
+      return { html: body?.innerHTML || '', text: body?.innerText || '', strongs, paras: (body?.querySelectorAll('.md-p') || []).length };
+    });
+    ok(info.strongs.length === 1 && info.strongs[0] === 'critical', `exactly one correct <strong>critical</strong> (got: ${JSON.stringify(info.strongs)})`);
+    ok(!/<strong>[^<]*<\/div>/.test(info.html) && !/<strong>[^<]*<div/.test(info.html), 'no <strong> bridges across a paragraph boundary (no </div>/<div> inside a strong)');
+    ok(info.paras >= 2, `two paragraphs render as separate .md-p blocks (got ${info.paras})`);
+    ok(/Warning: do not delete/.test(info.text) && /critical/.test(info.text), 'both paragraphs are present');
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${JSON.stringify(errs.slice(0, 2))})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projectDir); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();

+/**
+ * Verifies the UI's markdown renderer (renderInline) turns GitHub-flavored
+ * tables into real <table class="md"> elements — checked in a REAL browser
+ * against the served ui.html, so it exercises the actual shipped function.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-markdown-table.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 28899;
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) { if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label); } }
+function findChrome() {
+  return ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe']
+    .find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const chrome = findChrome();
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-mdtbl-'));
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; } },
+    modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: dir,
+  });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => typeof window.renderInline === 'function', { timeout: 10000 });
+    const run = (md) => page.evaluate((m) => window.renderInline(m), md);
+
+    console.log('\n[1] basic table → <table class="md"> with th + td');
+    let h = await run('| A | B |\n| --- | --- |\n| 1 | 2 |\n| 3 | 4 |');
+    ok(/<table class="md">/.test(h), 'table element emitted');
+    ok(/<th>A<\/th><th>B<\/th>/.test(h), 'header cells');
+    ok(/<td>1<\/td><td>2<\/td>/.test(h) && /<td>3<\/td><td>4<\/td>/.test(h), 'data rows');
+
+    console.log('\n[2] alignment via :--- / ---: / :--:');
+    h = await run('| L | C | R |\n|:---|:--:|---:|\n| a | b | c |');
+    ok(/text-align:left/.test(h) && /text-align:center/.test(h) && /text-align:right/.test(h), 'left/center/right alignments applied');
+
+    console.log('\n[3] inline bold/code inside cells');
+    h = await run('| Name | Note |\n|---|---|\n| **x** | `c` |');
+    ok(/<strong>x<\/strong>/.test(h) && /<code>c<\/code>/.test(h), 'bold + code rendered inside cells');
+
+    console.log('\n[3a] an escaped pipe inside code in a cell');
+    {
+      // (live round 10: a table cell showed `if (!a \|\| b)` - the code span was put away before the row was split,
+      // so its \| was never unescaped. GFM: in a table, \| is a | even inside code)
+      h = await run('| File | Fix |\n|---|---|\n| delete.js | `if (!id \\|\\| !ok)` |');
+      ok(/<code>if \(!id \|\| !ok\)<\/code>/.test(h), `\\| inside code in a cell renders as | (${(h.match(/<code>[^<]*<\/code>/) || [''])[0]})`);
+      ok(/<td>delete\.js<\/td>/.test(h) && (h.match(/<td/g) || []).length === 2, 'and the row still has its two cells');
+      const g = await run('Outside a table, `a \\| b` stays as written.');
+      ok(/<code>a \\\| b<\/code>/.test(g), 'outside a table the backslash is kept');
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[3b] <br> inside a cell breaks the line instead of printing the tag');
+    {
+      // Reported from real use: literal "<br>" showing in rendered tables. A cell cannot contain a newline, so
+      // <br> is the only way to break a line inside one — markdown has no other syntax for it, and the model uses
+      // it correctly. The renderer escapes everything up front, so the cell held "&lt;br&gt;" and printed it.
+      h = await run('| Step | Detail |\n|---|---|\n| one | first<br>second |');
+      ok(/first<br>second/.test(h), 'the break renders as a real <br>');
+      ok(!/&lt;br/.test(h), 'and the literal tag is gone');
+      for (const v of ['<br/>', '<br />', '<BR>']) {
+        const g = await run('| A |\n|---|\n| x' + v + 'y |');
+        ok(/x<br>y/.test(g), `the ${v} spelling works too`);
+      }
+      // Scoped to cells on purpose: the conversion undoes escaping, and doing it everywhere would hand the model
+      // a way to put raw HTML on the page.
+      const outside = await run('a line with <br> in ordinary prose');
+      ok(/&lt;br&gt;/.test(outside), 'outside a table it is still escaped, so this is not a general HTML hole');
+      const inj = await run('| A |\n|---|\n| <img src=x onerror=alert(1)><br>ok |');
+      ok(!/<img/.test(inj) && /&lt;img/.test(inj), 'and nothing else in the same cell escapes with it');
+
+      // A LITERAL <br> still has to be sayable. Backticks are how markdown already marks something as literal,
+      // and it is what the model writes when it MEANS the tag — a row documenting HTML is a real case for a
+      // coding agent, and silently turning that row into a line break would be worse than the bug being fixed.
+      const doc = await run('| Tag | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `<br>` | a line break |');
+      ok(/<code>&lt;br&gt;<\/code>/.test(doc), 'a code-spanned <br> stays literal');
+      ok(!/<code><br><\/code>/.test(doc), 'and is not turned into a break inside the code span');
+      // Both in one cell: the code span survives, the bare one still breaks.
+      const both = await run('| A |\n|---|\n| write `<br>` here<br>like this |');
+      ok(/<code>&lt;br&gt;<\/code>/.test(both) && /here<br>like/.test(both), 'both behaviours coexist in one cell');
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[4] a lone pipe line is NOT a table');
+    h = await run('see foo | bar then more text');
+    ok(!/<table/.test(h), 'no false-positive table without a delimiter row');
+
+    console.log('\n[5] table renders as a real DOM table when injected into the page');
+    const cellCount = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const d = document.createElement('div');
+      d.innerHTML = window.renderInline('| A | B |\n|---|---|\n| 1 | 2 |');
+      const t = d.querySelector('table.md');
+      return t ? t.querySelectorAll('td').length : -1;
+    });
+    ok(cellCount === 2, `injected table has 2 <td> cells (got ${cellCount})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[6] plain text still passes through unchanged');
+    h = await run('hello\nworld');
+    ok(!/<table/.test(h) && /hello\nworld/.test(h), 'non-table text untouched');
+
+    console.log('\n[7] markdown chars INSIDE an inline code span stay literal (the bug)');
+    h = await run('Use `# Comment` for a comment, `- x` for a list, `a | b` for a pipe');
+    ok(!/<h[1-6]/.test(h), 'no heading from a # inside backticks');
+    ok(!/<ul|<li/.test(h), 'no bullet from a - inside backticks');
+    ok(!/<table/.test(h), 'no table from a | inside backticks');
+    ok(/<code># Comment<\/code>/.test(h), `# rendered literally in a code span (got: ${h})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[8] fenced ``` block keeps #, -, | literal — one <pre><code>');
+    h = await run('```js\n# not a heading\n- not a bullet\na | b not a table\n```');
+    ok(/<pre class="md">(<button[^>]*class="code-copy"[^>]*>[^<]*<\/button>)?<code>/.test(h), 'fenced block rendered as <pre><code> (with a copy button)');
+    ok(!/<h[1-6]/.test(h) && !/<ul|<li/.test(h) && !/<table/.test(h), 'no heading/bullet/table from fenced content');
+    ok(/# not a heading/.test(h) && /a \| b not a table/.test(h), 'hash + pipe kept literal inside the block');
+
+    console.log('\n[9] heading WITH inline code still renders; multiple spans on a line');
+    h = await run('# Title with `code`');
+    ok(/<h3 class="md">Title with <code>code<\/code><\/h3>/.test(h), `heading keeps its inline code (got: ${h})`);
+    h = await run('run `a` then `b`');
+    ok((h.match(/<code>/g) || []).length === 2, 'two separate code spans on one line');
+
+    console.log('\n[10] **bold** inside backticks is NOT bolded');
+    h = await run('literal `**not bold**` here');
+    ok(/<code>\*\*not bold\*\*<\/code>/.test(h) && !/<strong>/.test(h), 'asterisks stay literal inside code');
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close();
+    server.close();
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });
